**作者:** @itxabdullaa | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/itxabdullaa/status/2102609881677107243) | **公開日:** 2026-09-23 | **ヒート:** 86

<summary><b>プロンプト全文（49 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
This AI is just cooking it 🔥 

Seedance 2.5 on @ImagineArt_X @imagineart_creo

Prompt 👇🏻

SCENE — KATEN KYŌKOTSU: KARAMATSU SHINJŪ
DURATION: exactly 10 seconds
FORMAT: 16:9
STYLE: ultra-photorealistic live-action cinematic fantasy action, grounded CGI, desaturated palette, high-contrast lighting, realistic anatomy and physics, dark supernatural atmosphere, cinematic Japanese tragedy, practical environmental interaction, no digital-art appearance.
PACE: extremely fast continuous escalation, zero pause, zero dead frame.

REFERENCES
IMAGE 1 — PAKSHUN
Preserve facial identity, hairstyle, costume, proportions, and appearance exactly.

IMAGE 2 — OPPONENT
Preserve facial identity, hairstyle, costume, proportions, and appearance exactly.

ACTION

0.0–2.0 SECONDS — BANKAI
Tight rear three-quarter shot. Opponent charges. Pakshun lowers his sword and says: 「卍解……花天狂骨枯松心中。」
A violent pressure wave tears across the ground. Dust, leaves, debris, and hundreds of dark petals rise. The environment rapidly desaturates into black, grey, and pale tones. Camera performs a fast 360° orbit as Opponent closes in.

2.0–4.0 SECONDS — SHARED WOUND
Opponent attacks. Pakshun pivots and cuts across his torso. An identical wound instantly appears on Pakshun. A thin crimson thread connects both wounds as Opponent retreats. The ground cracks beneath it and black water erupts upward.

4.0–6.5 SECONDS — THE DROWNED STAGE
A massive wave floods the battlefield. Camera dives underwater seamlessly. Both characters struggle violently against the current, with realistic movement of soaked clothing, hair, debris, and petals. Pakshun plants his feet and pulls one hand back. The entire current suddenly freezes in turbulent suspension.

6.5–8.5 SECONDS — WHITE THREADS
Pakshun rises through the suspended water. Countless thin white threads spread behind him into a huge web. Opponent tries to escape, but threads catch his wrist, arms, torso, and legs, tightening and dragging him toward the center. Pakshun advances. Camera rapidly pulls back to reveal the full structure.

8.5–10.0 SECONDS — HAKKA NO TOGAME
Pakshun grips one primary thread and pulls once. Every thread becomes violently taut. Water erupts outward and dark petals explode across the frame. Pakshun makes a minimal sword-draw motion. One sharp white cutting line tears across the composition.

SMASH CUT TO BLACK. One sharp sword-slash sound. END.

VISUAL RULES

- Both characters remain active throughout.
- No teleportation, transformations, NPC behavior, or static posing.
- No glowing anime aura or excessive energy beams.
- All water, debris, fabric, hair, petals, and threads obey believable physics.
- Grounded CGI integrated into live-action photography.
- Every action directly causes the next event.
- Continuous movement, zero pauses, zero dead frames.
- No unnecessary movement or ending hero pose. #ImagineArt
```

</details>

Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic Korean subway action-comedy scene using the uploaded images as exact visual references; keep characters, faces, hair, outfits and environment consistent.
Young Korean woman, early 20s, long straight black hair, fitted pastel-blue cropped top, loose pajama-style pants, simple silver jewelry; confident, expressive and slightly mischievous.
Young Korean man, short black hair, gray button-up shirt, dark trousers and white sneakers; playful at first, then shocked and embarrassed.
She rushes down the subway stairs toward a waiting train, but the doors close just as she reaches them.
The man inside sees her through the glass and laughs at her; she stares at him angrily and says, “Are you seriously laughing?”
He laughs again, so she suddenly punches the glass beside the door, cracking and breaking the safety glass realistically with no injury or gore.
She reaches through the broken opening, grabs his shirt and pulls him safely out onto the platform; he lands awkwardly on the floor in shock.
She calmly climbs through the same broken opening into the train, straightens her pastel top and pajama pants, and walks inside as stunned passengers watch.
The man remains sitting outside, looking confused and humiliated while passengers stare at him through the windows.
One passenger starts laughing, then everyone bursts into laughter while the man looks around helplessly.
She looks at him through the window, gives a tiny satisfied smile, then casually turns away.
Ultra-realistic Korean subway lighting, gritty DV-inspired look, natural motion blur, realistic physics, handheld imperfections, authentic reactions and ambient subway audio; no music.
No gore, blood, serious injuries, identity drift, outfit changes, duplicated people, teleportation, regenerating glass, distorted hands, CGI look, subtitles, captions, text, logos or watermark.
```

### A Cat's Cozy Day Filmed as a Selfie Vlog

> Just a normal day in the life of a very busy cat 🐱✨ Created with Seedance 2.5 on @wavespeed_ai Prompt: Create a 60-seco

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/media/poster/zarairahh-seedance-ai-f89372941867.jpg" width="600" alt="A Cat's Cozy Day Filmed as a Selfie Vlog">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/zarairahh-seedance-ai-f89372941867?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @ZaraIrahh | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/ZaraIrahh/status/2091385137133219971) | **公開日:** 2026-08-23 | **ヒート:** 86

**安定度:** 70/100

**再テスト:** MiniMax H3 Max 768p · 2026-09-07 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 70.2) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/zarairahh-seedance-ai-f89372941867/video-minimax-h3-768p-20260907-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（116 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 60-second realistic day-in-the-life cat vlog, following the same cute cat from waking up in the morning until falling asleep at night.

Critical Requirement

NO HUMAN SPEECH AND NO DIALOGUE.

The cat must not speak any words at any point. Do not use narration, voice-over, human voices, subtitles, captions, or text.

The story must be communicated entirely through the cat’s actions, facial expressions, body language, meows, purrs, chirps, yawns, and natural environmental sounds.

Overall Visual Style

One continuous handheld front-facing smartphone selfie-vlog perspective. The cat holds the phone itself with its paw and films its own day.

- Realistic modern smartphone footage
- Natural handheld shake
- Slight framing imperfections
- Occasional autofocus hunting
- Natural front-camera lens distortion
- Realistic unfiltered smartphone colors
- Soft natural morning, afternoon, and evening lighting
- Same cat, face, fur pattern, eyes, and body throughout
- Cute but believable cat behavior
- Natural expressions and movements
- No cinematic camera movements
- No third-person camera
- No tripod or external camera
- No beauty filter or cinematic color grading
- No logos, watermarks, subtitles, captions, or on-screen text
- No background music

0–10 sec — Wake Up

The cat wakes up in a cozy bed.

It picks up the phone with its paw and starts filming itself selfie-style.

It looks sleepy, yawns, stretches, rubs its face with its paws, and looks into the camera with sleepy eyes.

It gives a tiny “meow”, then climbs out of bed.

10–20 sec — Breakfast

The cat walks into the kitchen while filming itself.

It sees its food bowl and immediately becomes excited.

It prepares or receives its breakfast, eagerly eats, then drinks water.

It looks happily into the camera and gives a couple of excited little meows.

Include realistic food-bowl sounds, chewing, drinking, and kitchen ambience.

20–30 sec — Playtime

The cat grabs its favorite toy.

It runs around the house, chases the toy, jumps onto the couch, bats at it with its paws, and playfully rolls around.

The phone shakes naturally during the energetic movement.

The cat stops for a moment, looks directly into the camera, gives a playful chirp/meow, then immediately runs off again.

30–40 sec — Exploring

The cat explores the house.

It looks through a window, watches birds outside, investigates a strange sound, walks into another room, and sniffs interesting objects.

It notices something outside and becomes curious.

The cat makes a soft “mrrp” sound and continues exploring.

Keep the behavior authentically feline rather than human-like.

40–50 sec — Afternoon Adventure

The cat safely goes outside into a garden or quiet courtyard.

It walks around while filming itself, sniffs flowers and plants, watches birds, investigates leaves, and explores its surroundings.

The cat pauses, looks excitedly at something off-camera, then gives a small curious meow.

Use natural outdoor sounds such as birds, wind, leaves, distant traffic, and footsteps.

50–60 sec — Evening & Bedtime

The cat returns home as the light becomes warm and soft.

It has its evening meal, drinks some water, then climbs onto the couch or back into its bed.

It grooms itself, yawns, becomes sleepy, and curls into a comfortable position.

It looks into the phone one last time, gives a tiny sleepy meow, slowly closes its eyes, and lowers the phone naturally.

End with quiet nighttime home ambience.

Final Audio Requirements

Absolutely no dialogue. No words. No narration. No human voice. No talking cat.

Use only natural diegetic sounds:

- Cat meows
- Purring
- Chirping
- Yawning
- Footsteps
- Food and water sounds
- Toy sounds
- Household ambience
- Birds and outdoor sounds
- Wind and leaves
- Natural nighttime ambience

The finished video should feel like a realistic, cozy smartphone vlog from a cat’s perspective, showing what the cat naturally does throughout an entire day without saying a single word.
```

</details>

```
0–3 sec — Hook
A young wizard stands frozen in a dark forest as a massive monster charges toward her.
Wizard: “Ten seconds… that’s all I need!”

3–8 sec — The Spell
She raises her glowing staff and rapidly chants a magical spell. The monster gets closer and closer.

8–12 sec — Countdown
Monster: “YOU’RE TOO LATE!”
The wizard finishes the final word: “TRANSFORMUS!”

12–16 sec — Magic Burst
A huge explosion of purple light fills the screen. Everything goes silent.

16–21 sec — Twist
The smoke clears. The monster looks confused.
Instead of the wizard… there’s a tiny, adorable duck standing in her robes.

21–26 sec — Reaction
The duck looks down at herself, quacks angrily, and smacks the staff with her wing.

26–30 sec — Punchline
The monster slowly backs away.
Monster: “...Honestly, I’m scared of her.”
The duck suddenly starts chasing him.
SFX: Quack! Quack! Quack!
End text:
✨ Never rush a spell. 🦆

### Gym selfie vlog of jump-roping, then a water break

> Made with Seedance 2.5 on @makeugc (native 15-second video, no editing) PROMPT ⬇️ **CAMERA:** DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld feel. A self-filmed gym vlog: MILA…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/72aa1a823671.jpg" width="600" alt="Gym selfie vlog of jump-roping, then a water break">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-dv-16mm-tape-camcorder-handheld-feel-2220cc5e7adc?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @YourAlphaMom | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/YourAlphaMom/status/2121715884666360217) | **公開日:** 2026-11-14 | **ヒート:** 85

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（55 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
**CAMERA:**
DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld feel. A self-filmed gym vlog: MILA holds the camera for the opening selfie, then props it securely on a low bench for the jump-rope shots. Slight hand shake, misaligned framing, delayed autofocus and a briefly cropped forehead in the selfie. Once propped, the camera stays still; tighter inserts are simple crops of that same recording. Casual hard cuts omit setup time. The camcorder never appears on screen or in the mirror.

**LOOK:**
Soft, slightly blurry tape quality, faint tape noise, bloomed highlights under gym lighting, subtle auto-exposure fluctuations, muted contrast, realistic skin tones. MILA looks like a real woman recorded in an ordinary gym: natural skin texture, small imperfections, fine loose hairs and spontaneous facial expressions remain visible beneath the gentle tape softness. Soft overhead lighting creates believable shadows beneath her chin and feet. No beauty-filter smoothing or glossy commercial grading.

**STYLE:**
Playful, self-deprecating gym-vlog tone — initial confidence, a few genuinely good jumps, one tiny mistake and an extremely well-earned water break. Real effort mixed with humor. Short, casual cuts, normal-speed movement and a light, funny finish.

**Character**

MILA — a beautiful American Instagram-style brunette model and performer, 20. Long dark-brown hair in a high ponytail, glowing skin with a light sweat sheen, glossy lips, expressive eyes, soft glam makeup and delicate sparkly stud earrings. Slim athletic figure with a very large, full bust, very full rounded buttocks, a narrow waist, flat stomach and lean arms and legs.

She wears a cobalt-blue supportive sports bra with thin straps and a deep scoop neckline showing cleavage, paired with matching ultra-short fitted athletic shorts. Bare shoulders, arms and midriff. White ankle socks, sneakers off. She speaks with an American accent.

Her ponytail follows each jump with a slight natural delay. Her shoulders stay relaxed, knees soften on landing, and her fitted sportswear moves naturally with her body.

**Setting**

A gym mat area in the evening, with a flat rubber exercise floor, a mirror wall off to one side and other equipment visible in the background. In shots 2–4, the camera looks diagonally toward the mirror so MILA's full-length reflection is clearly visible beside her, matching her movements and the rope on every turn, including the criss-cross jump. Soft overhead lighting. Across the aisle, one gym member pedals a stationary bike and another does seated cable rows. Beside the bench, well outside the rope's path, stands a half-full, store-bought 500 ml bottle of water. It has thin clear ribbed plastic, a narrow neck and a small white screw cap. One ordinary dark jump rope with two black handles. Enough clear space above and around her for the full rope arc.

**Storyboard (15s, 6 shots)**

1. *(0–2s, handheld arm's-length selfie)*  
MILA looks into the lens with an overly confident little smile, holding both rope handles together in her free hand. One loose strand of hair rests against her cheek. The framing dips slightly as she starts lowering the camera toward the bench.  
MILA: “This looks easy.”

2. *(2–5s, propped camera, full-body shot)*  
Hard cut: the camera is now resting on the bench, and MILA is already jumping a few steps away. Her whole body, both handles and the rope's overhead arc fit in frame. She makes several small, steady two-foot hops, turning the rope with compact wrist movements, elbows close to her sides. The bottle stands at the edge of frame. Several metres behind MILA, both gym members are visible at their machines, each absorbed in their own workout. Her ponytail bobs behind her.  
No dialogue — rhythmic rope taps, soft landings and light breathing.

3. *(5–7s, slightly tighter full-body crop from the same propped recording)*  
On one turn, as the rope swings down in front of her, MILA crosses her forearms low at her waist, right wrist over left. She clears the crossed loop with one small two-foot hop. She opens her arms on the next turn and clears that one cleanly too. Both wrists and both feet stay in frame. A quick, pleased grin.  
No dialogue. Two evenly spaced taps of the rope on the rubber floor.

4. *(7–9s, back to the full-body framing)*  
The rope catches lightly against her toes on the next pass. Its rotation stops; she settles onto both feet and takes one small recovery step, still holding a handle in each hand. She looks down at the slack rope, then back toward the lens with an amused, mildly offended expression.  
MILA, with a breathy little laugh: “Okay. Rude.”

5. *(9–11.5s, propped camera, medium crop near the bench)*  
Jump cut: she is crouched beside the bench. She places both handles and the slack rope on the floor, then picks up the same water bottle with her left hand and twists off its loosely fitted cap with her right. The cap stays between her fingers. Her shoulders rise and fall with slightly quicker breathing; she smiles to herself.  
No dialogue. The thin plastic crinkles softly in her grip over the hum of the exercise bike.

6. *(11.5–15s, closer crop, camera still propped)*  
Still crouching near the lens, she brings the bottle's narrow open neck to her lips, tips it for one small sip, lowers it and swallows. The water tilts naturally inside the bottle. She lets out a small satisfied breath, looks straight into the camera and gives a cheeky half-smile.  
MILA: “Still counts.”  
Finish on her amused expression, with the bottle lowered in her hand.

**Audio and continuity**

Natural American-accented speech with accurate lip sync. Soft gym room tone, rope swishes and floor taps synchronized with each rotation, light breathing, the soft crinkle of the water bottle and a quiet sip. Leave space between actions and dialogue. No music, voiceover, captions or added comedy effects.

Keep MILA's face, proportions, outfit, hairstyle, lighting and surroundings consistent. The rope remains one continuous cord attached to the same two handles: one rotation per hop, no extra loops, disappearing sections or passage through her body. Both hands hold their respective handles throughout the jumping. The same bottle stays beside the bench until she visibly picks it up.

#AIVideo
```

</details>

### Schoolgirl falls while fleeing a wild boar on a wet highway

> The highway just became a death trap. Seedance 2.5 on @dreamina_ai #dreamina #dreaminacreators prompt MAIN CHARACTER — STRICT IDENTITY LOCK A beautiful 22-year-…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/81863408dd07.jpg" width="600" alt="Schoolgirl falls while fleeing a wild boar on a wet highway">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-the-highway-just-became-a-death-trap-21a518f1cfd0?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Just_sharon7 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Just_sharon7/status/2120526947415703565) | **公開日:** 2026-11-11 | **ヒート:** 85

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（167 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
The highway just became a death trap.

Seedance 2.5 on @dreamina_ai 

#dreamina #dreaminacreators 

prompt

MAIN CHARACTER — STRICT IDENTITY LOCK
A beautiful 22-year-old Korean schoolgirl with long golden-blonde hair, wearing a dark navy-blue school blazer, a white button-up shirt, a red striped tie, a black-and-red plaid mini skirt, white socks, and black school shoes.
Her face is filled with terror. Her breathing is rapid, her eyes are wide with fear, and she is desperately running for her life.
Her golden-blonde hair flies wildly behind her as she sprints. Her blazer, tie, and skirt react naturally to her movements and the wind.
STRICT IDENTITY CONSISTENCY: Maintain exactly the same face, hairstyle, outfit, body proportions, and facial features throughout the entire sequence.

SCENE 01 — 0:00–0:05 | HIGHWAY PANIC

Camera: Low-angle cinematic side-tracking shot, transitioning into a fast handheld chase.

A massive highway is completely blocked by hundreds of abandoned vehicles. The sky is covered with thick gray clouds, and the asphalt is wet from recent rainfall.

The Korean schoolgirl sprints desperately between stationary cars, trying to escape.

Suddenly, an enormous, aggressive wild boar bursts out from between two vehicles.

The creature has a massive muscular body, muddy dark-brown fur, visible scratches, enormous curved tusks, and terrifying natural animal movements.
It violently crashes into a black sedan, smashing its bumper and shattering the windows.

Glass fragments scatter across the wet highway.

The girl hears the impact and turns her head in terror.

The camera rapidly whip-pans from the destroyed vehicle back to her frightened face.

SCENE 02 — 0:05–0:10 | FACE TO FACE WITH DEATH

Camera: Front-facing tracking shot moving backward at the girl's running speed.

The girl sprints between two parked SUVs.

Suddenly, the gigantic wild boar charges directly toward her from behind.

Its powerful hooves slam against the wet asphalt, sending water splashing in every direction.

The creature runs with realistic animal biomechanics, its massive shoulders moving violently with every stride.

The girl looks backward for a split second.
Her eyes widen in absolute terror.

She screams and accelerates, desperately trying to outrun the enormous animal.

The camera remains extremely close to her face while the wild boar rapidly closes the distance behind her.

SCENE 03 — 0:10–0:15 | THE NEAR-FATAL ATTACK

Camera: Aggressive over-the-shoulder tracking shot, followed by a sudden ground-level perspective.

The wild boar launches a terrifying forward charge.

Its enormous tusks narrowly miss the girl's body, tearing the fabric of her blazer as it passes.

The sudden movement causes her to lose her balance.

She stumbles forward and crashes onto the wet asphalt.

Her hands strike the road first, absorbing the impact.

Her golden-blonde hair falls across her face, and her school uniform reacts naturally to the fall.
The camera rapidly drops to ground level, capturing the impact with intense realism.

In the background, several additional wild boars emerge from between the abandoned vehicles.

Car alarms echo across the highway.

SCENE 04 — 0:15–0:21 | DESPERATE SURVIVAL

Camera: Low-angle handheld close-up transitioning into a fast backward-tracking shot.

The girl struggles to get up from the slippery road.

Her palms slide across the wet asphalt.

She pushes herself onto one knee and looks backward.

Three enormous wild boars are charging directly toward her at full speed.

Their heavy footsteps shake the ground.

Water splashes beneath their hooves.

The girl's expression changes from fear to pure survival instinct.
She forces herself upright and immediately begins sprinting again.

The camera races backward in front of her, capturing her terrified expression while the charging animals remain visible behind her.

Abandoned vehicles, shattered glass, and flashing hazard lights create an intense disaster-movie atmosphere.

SCENE 05 — 0:21–0:26 | THE TRUCK ESCAPE

Camera: Fast side-tracking shot transitioning into an extremely low ground-level perspective.

The girl notices a large black cargo truck stopped across the highway.

The wild boars are rapidly approaching from behind.

With no other escape route, she lowers her body and performs a desperate sliding maneuver beneath the stationary truck.

Her hands and knees slide across the wet asphalt.

The camera follows her underneath the vehicle, remaining only inches above the road.

She pulls herself deeper beneath the truck, keeping her body as low as possible.

An enormous wild boar charges past the outside of the truck.

Its massive hooves thunder dangerously close to the camera.
The girl freezes, terrified of being discovered.

SCENE 06 — 0:26–0:30 | FINAL SUSPENSE

Camera: Extreme close-up beneath the truck, slowly pushing toward the girl's face.

The girl lies motionless beneath the stationary cargo truck.

Her golden-blonde hair is scattered across her frightened face.

Her breathing is rapid and uneven.

She desperately tries to remain silent.

Suddenly, a gigantic wild boar stops directly outside the truck.

Its heavy breathing becomes clearly audible.

The creature begins sniffing the ground.

The girl slowly turns her head toward the sound.

A massive animal shadow moves across her face.
The camera pushes into an extreme close-up of her terrified eyes.

FINAL SHOT: The wild boar lowers its enormous head toward the opening beneath the truck.

Its curved tusks become visible in the darkness.

The girl holds her breath.

SUDDEN CUT TO BLACK.

CINEMATOGRAPHY & VISUAL EFFECTS

Ultra-realistic live-action Korean disaster thriller, Hollywood-level visual effects, physically accurate wild boar anatomy, natural animal running mechanics, realistic human movement, aggressive handheld cinematography, fast tracking shots, intense low-angle perspectives, dynamic camera movement, cinematic motion blur, wet asphalt reflections, overcast daylight, realistic vehicle destruction, physically accurate glass fragmentation, detailed fur simulation, natural hair movement, realistic fabric physics, atmospheric depth, dramatic shadows, high-contrast cinematic color grading, photorealistic skin textures, seamless scene continuity, 4K resolution, 60 FPS.

Use brief 120 FPS slow-motion inserts only for the near-miss attack, the girl's fall, and the slide beneath the truck. All other action must play at natural real-time speed.

SOUND DESIGN

Powerful cinematic orchestral percussion, aggressive wild boar growls, heavy hooves striking wet asphalt, car alarms, shattering glass, distant human screams, splashing water, rapid footsteps, terrified breathing, deep cinematic bass impacts, and rising suspense music.

The music gradually intensifies throughout the chase, then becomes almost completely silent during the final moments beneath the truck.

End with a sudden deep bass impact followed by complete silence.

STRICT NEGATIVE PROMPT

No cartoon physics, no unrealistic animal anatomy, no floating bodies, no exaggerated jumping, no unnatural running, no teleportation, no duplicated characters, no extra limbs, no distorted hands, no facial identity changes, no hairstyle changes, no inconsistent clothing, no unrealistic body movements, no artificial-looking CGI, no sudden environment changes, no camera clipping, no excessive gore, no random scene transitions.

FINAL GENERATION RULE: Maintain maximum photorealism, continuous character identity, accurate environmental physics, realistic animal behavior, intense cinematic pacing, and seamless visual continuity throughout the entire 30-second sequence.

Dale the werewolf: a huge, hulking, shaggy grey-brown werewolf with glowing yellow eyes, a long snarling snout full of teeth, and matted fur. He wears a torn red varsity-style bowling jacket hanging off his massive frame, shredded at the sleeves. The skeletons: a trio of full-body human skeletons with bleached off-white bones, wearing mirrored aviator sunglasses and black tactical vests. One of them wears a bright orange baseball cap and white sneakers. The elderly witch: a frail, wrinkled old woman with wild grey hair, wearing a black feathered cape and sharp cat-eye glasses, with a pointed witch's hat and a crooked grin. The Headless Horseman: a tall rider in a burgundy velvet coat with no head, seated on a powerful black horse with a flowing mane. Shot 1 (0-2.2s): Flash-lit fisheye shot at a gas station at night, the devil woman bored against an old pickup, camera close on her face; out of the black behind her, the werewolf sprints into the flash and leaps straight at the lens; she swings up a shotgun and fires, muzzle flash whiting out the frame, camera knocked sideways. Shot 2 (2.2-2.8s): Whip-pan into a dark alley: three skeletons on a low wall lit only by the flash, one raises his rifle and they jump down at the camera. Shot 3 (2.8-3.6s): The devil woman leaps out of a black archway right past the lens, her red tulle skirt and sheer sleeves flaring and filling the frame, firing a rifle. Shot 4 (3.6-4s): Flash-blown close-up of muzzle flash and sparks, camera shaking. Shot 5 (4-5s): Running handheld through a dark farmyard toward a shed with propane tanks; the elderly witch zooms past the lens on a ride-on lawnmower, cape flapping, straight into the tanks. 

Shot 6 (5-6s): Massive fireball overexposes the camera; the witch is blasted up into the black sky; operator falls back. Shot 7 (6-6.6s): Pitch black cornfield, flash pops on: her broom stabs into the dirt right in front of the lens. Shot 8 (6.6-7.8s): 

City street at night, camera on the sidewalk; the Headless Horseman gallops inches past the fisheye lens holding a flaming jack-o'-lantern, fire flaring the image. Shot 9 (7.8-8.8s): 

A parked old sedan explodes, camera recoils, image clips to white then auto-exposes back. Shot 10 (8.8-12.2s): Packed barn Halloween party, flash on, crowd in witch hats and bedsheet ghosts screaming and holding up phones. 

Dance battle filmed from the front row: the devil woman, in her full devil costume with horns and heels, hits a sharp freeze right in the fisheye lens; the skeleton in the orange cap and white sneakers does a headspin, then collapses into a pile of loose bones. 

Crowd goes insane, camera shaking. Shot 11 (12.2-14s): Camera drops to the floor: harsh flash on scattered bones, the skull rolls straight into the lens and stops, distorted by the fisheye, background black. Hold. ```
Create a 30-second, photorealistic live-action miniature-person VFX comedy video showing an ordinary shower turning into an extreme survival adventure. Use cinematic macro photography, ultra-shallow depth of field, realistic water physics, dynamic handheld camera movements, dramatic lighting, and spectacular scale contrast. The video should feel like a premium Hollywood miniature-action sequence filmed inside a real bathroom, with hilarious near-disasters and visually satisfying moments. Ultra-detailed 4K quality, realistic textures, natural motion blur, and physically believable interactions.

CHARACTER CONSISTENCY

The main character is a tiny adult woman approximately 1:5 human scale, with realistic facial features, expressive eyes, shoulder-length dark hair tied in a small ponytail, a pastel-blue sleeveless top, dark shorts, and tiny white sneakers. Keep her face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and scale perfectly consistent throughout the entire video. She must look like a real miniature person, not an animated or cartoon character.

SCENE SEQUENCE

Scene 1 — The Danger Begins
A tiny woman stands on the edge of a bathroom floor beside a giant shower area, confidently preparing for a shower. The camera slowly pushes in as she looks up at the enormous showerhead above her. Suddenly, the shower turns on, and her confident expression changes to pure terror.

Scene 2 — The First Waterfall
A massive stream of water crashes down like a powerful waterfall. She runs for cover as giant droplets explode against the tiles around her. One enormous droplet splashes beside her, sending her tumbling backward across the wet floor.

Scene 3 — Soap Bar Avalanche
She climbs onto a giant soap bar to escape the rushing water. The slippery soap suddenly slides across the floor like a runaway skateboard. She desperately balances on top, waving her arms while racing toward the drain.

Scene 4 — The Shampoo Bottle Collapse
She jumps off the soap bar and grabs the edge of a giant shampoo bottle for safety. The bottle wobbles, tips over, and releases a thick stream of shampoo onto the floor. She slides down the slippery shampoo trail like a water slide, barely avoiding a giant puddle.

Scene 5 — The Drain Escape
The water begins flowing rapidly toward the bathroom drain. She runs in the opposite direction as tiny waves chase her feet. At the last moment, she leaps onto a raised tile edge and grabs a small grout line, dangling dramatically above the rushing water.

Scene 6 — The Final Victory
She pulls herself onto a dry bathmat, exhausted but triumphant. Behind her, the shower suddenly releases one final giant splash that sends her flying forward onto the soft mat. She slowly lifts her head, gives the showerhead an angry look, and shakes her fist at it. End with a dramatic close-up of her soaked, furious face.

CAMERA AND VISUAL STYLE

- Cinematic macro close-ups emphasizing the tiny woman's scale against oversized bathroom objects.
- Low-angle tracking shots following her frantic escape.
- Fast whip pans, subtle handheld shake, and brief slow motion during major splashes and jumps.
- Realistic water droplets, splashes, wet surfaces, reflections, and slippery soap physics.
- Sharp focus on the character with beautiful background bokeh.
- Comedic timing, expressive facial reactions, and seamless transitions between action beats.
- Maintain consistent bathroom layout, lighting, character appearance, and object positions.

SOUND DESIGN

Use synchronized, exaggerated but realistic sound effects: shower valve click, thunderous rushing water, heavy splashes, slippery soap squeaks, shampoo bottle thud, wet sliding sounds, frantic footsteps, dramatic whooshes, and a final soft bathmat landing. Add playful, suspenseful instrumental background music that builds during the chase and ends with a funny musical sting.
```

Walked past him like the backflip was the easy part of lunch.

Seedance 2.5 on @FishCreativeHQ 

Prompt

Dozens of students in pastel blue short-sleeve summer uniforms with purple ties (boys) and pink ribbon bows (girls) eating from stainless-steel compartment trays.
Heroine: early-20s Korean woman @[Image 1](image_1) styled as a high-school senior — long straight pastel pink hair with center part and face-framing layers, pale makeup, glossy lips, pastel pink short-sleeve school blouse, navy ribbon bow, pastel pink mini skirt, white crew socks, chunky white sneakers, small blue name tag on her chest. She walks the center aisle carrying a stainless 5-compartment lunch tray in both hands. Tray contents: mound of white rice, spicy red tteokbokki cylinders, a pile of kimchi, seasoned green namul, and a small metal soup bowl of red stew. Spoon and chopsticks rest on the tray.
Shot 1 (0–1s): Medium-wide tracking shot. She walks straight toward camera down the aisle, calm expression, tray held level at waist height. Background students laugh and eat.
Shot 2 (1s): Tight low insert of her feet. She scuffs the heel of her left white sneaker against the polished floor, then plants both feet.
Shot 3 (2s): Medium close-up of her face as she looks up, slightly startled, hair swinging.
Shot 4–6 (3–6s): Slow-motion VFX food sequence. She tosses the tray upward. The tray and utensils hang in mid-air. Tteokbokki pieces, kimchi, and namul lift off the compartments and float in a neat horizontal line above the tray, as if gravity paused. Cut to overhead: food settles perfectly back into each compartment; soup bowl stays upright, nothing spills.
Shot 7 (7s): Wide. She drops into a fast back handspring / cartwheel on the cafeteria floor, skirt flipping, one sneaker kicking high. Students at nearby tables freeze and stare.
Shot 8 (8s): She lands spinning, hair whipping, and catches the falling tray and utensils in one fluid motion without dropping food.
Shot 9–11 (9–11s): Close-ups of hands catching chopsticks and spoon, then a hero beauty shot of the completed tray held out toward camera — rice, tteokbokki, kimchi, namul, and stew all perfectly arranged.
Shot 12 (12s): Cut to a table of boys. Handsome dark-haired boy in white shirt and navy tie turns, mouth open in shock; friends around him gape.
Shot 13–14 (13–14s): Tight beauty close-up of the heroine’s face, staring into camera, then a tiny knowing smirk.
Shot 15–20 (15–20s): She walks past the shocked boy’s table, tray in her left hand, glancing down at him. Soft line of dialogue in Korean, subtitled: “Let’s eat and see.” He looks down, embarrassed. She keeps walking.
Shot 21 (21s): Rear tracking shot as she walks away down the aisle, long hair and navy skirt, cafeteria buzzing again behind her.
Style notes: high-end K-drama cinematography (something like a glossy teen romance / school fantasy), practical cafeteria set, subtle slow-motion only on the floating-food and flip beats, clean VFX so the food never smears or looks cartoonish, no logos, no on-screen text except optional Korean subtitle on the one line.
Negative: messy spills, broken tray, Western cafeteria, adult office clothes, exaggerated anime faces, shaky phone footage, watermarks.

One girl. One boat. A race against the final wave. 🌊🔥

Created with Seedance 2.5 on @dreamina_ai

 #dreamina #dreaminacreators

Prompt 
THE FINAL WAVE | A DESPERATE TSUNAMI RESCUE

Create a 30-second ultra-realistic live-action cinematic disaster video set in a Japanese coastal fishing village. A massive tsunami approaches after a powerful earthquake. A brave young woman risks her life to rescue stranded people.

CHARACTER & LOCATION:
A young Japanese woman with dark hair in a ponytail, wearing a dark waterproof jacket, black trousers and rubber boots. Three adult fishermen, one small child and one elderly woman. Keep all characters, outfits, faces, rescue boat and village consistent.

EXACT SCENE SEQUENCE:

1. EARTHQUAKE (0–3s):
The village shakes violently. Objects fall, houses rattle and villagers rush outside.

2. TSUNAMI WARNING (3–5s):
Emergency sirens sound. The girl looks towards the ocean as the water suddenly retreats from the harbor.

3. FISHERMEN IN DANGER (5–8s):
She spots three fishermen stranded near a damaged fishing boat, desperately waving for help.

4. RESCUE BOAT (8–11s):
She runs to a small motorboat, unties it and starts the engine.

5. ROUGH SEA (11–15s):
She navigates through violent waves, avoiding floating debris as water splashes over the boat.

6. FISHERMEN RESCUE (15–19s):
She reaches the damaged boat and helps all three fishermen climb safely aboard, catching one as he nearly slips.

7. CHILD AND ELDERLY WOMAN (19–22s):
She spots a frightened child and elderly woman trapped on a flooded rooftop and steers towards them.

8. MASSIVE TSUNAMI (22–26s):
A gigantic dark wave appears behind the harbor. The fishermen help bring the child and elderly woman aboard from a reachable flooded platform.

9. ESCAPE (26–28s):
The boat reaches elevated ground. She helps everyone climb to safety as floodwater crashes through the harbor.

10. FINAL SHOT (28–30s):
The rescued child holds her hand. She watches in shock as the massive wave engulfs the distant village.

VISUAL STYLE:
Ultra-photorealistic live-action disaster film, realistic tsunami physics, powerful water movement, natural handheld documentary camera, stormy daylight, wet clothing, emotional expressions and believable boat movement.

Natural audio: earthquake rumbling, emergency sirens, wind, engine, shouting and crashing waves. No music, subtitles or voiceover.

STRICT CONTINUITY:
Same characters, outfits, boat and location throughout. Show every rescue physically, with logical movement between scenes. No teleportation, superhero powers, cartoon water or unrealistic CGI.

The final video must feel like real disaster footage, full of tension, urgency and emotion.
```

```
Imagine being so dangerous that even explosions are just background effects.

Seedance 2.5 @FishCreativeHQ 

Prompt

VISUAL STYLE

Ultra-photorealistic Hollywood blockbuster cinematography. A dramatic nighttime explosion sequence featuring a mysterious young woman walking calmly toward the camera while a massive fireball erupts behind her. Realistic fire simulation, powerful shockwaves, thick smoke, flying debris, atmospheric lighting, cinematic contrast, natural skin textures, and realistic human movement. Cool blue nighttime tones contrast with intense orange flames.

CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK

A striking 24-year-old woman with long, straight pastel-pink hair and soft bangs framing her face. Pale natural skin, delicate facial features, and a cold, emotionless expression.

OUTFIT: A fitted rainbow-striped long-sleeve turtleneck shirt underneath faded light-blue denim short overalls, with a front bib pocket and adjustable shoulder straps. Red bands around both upper thighs. Barefoot.

Preserve her exact face, hairstyle, outfit, body proportions, and accessories throughout the entire video.

SETTING

An isolated, empty two-lane asphalt road at midnight. The road is slightly wet, reflecting the overhead streetlights. A faded yellow centerline stretches into the distance. Evenly spaced modern streetlights illuminate both sides of the road, disappearing into the darkness. No vehicles, pedestrians, or surrounding buildings.

CAMERA

One continuous frontal tracking shot. The camera moves backward at exactly the same speed as the woman, maintaining a centered, full-body composition. Smooth stabilized movement with subtle natural cinematic motion. 35mm lens, shallow atmospheric depth, realistic lighting, and high dynamic range.

ACTION TIMELINE

00:00–00:02 — THE MYSTERIOUS WALK

The woman walks barefoot directly toward the camera along the center of the deserted road. She casually holds a cigarette near her lips, takes a final drag, and maintains a completely emotionless expression. Her long pastel-pink hair moves gently in the nighttime breeze.

00:02–00:04 — THE IGNITION

She lowers her hand and continues walking without looking backward. A sudden bright blue-white flash illuminates the road far behind her. A small cloud of smoke begins expanding rapidly in the background.

00:04–00:06 — THE MASSIVE EXPLOSION

A gigantic orange fireball violently erupts behind the woman, filling the entire width of the road. The explosion expands upward into a towering mushroom-shaped cloud of flames and black smoke.

Powerful shockwaves push dust and debris outward. Bright orange firelight illuminates the woman's pink hair, denim overalls, and the reflective asphalt.

She never turns around, flinches, or changes her walking speed.

00:06–00:08 — THE UNTOUCHABLE WALK

The woman continues walking directly toward the camera with complete confidence. Behind her, enormous flames roll across the road while thick smoke rises into the night sky.

The explosion creates dramatic orange rim lighting around her silhouette. Her hair sways gently from the displaced air. Her facial expression remains cold and completely indifferent.

00:08–00:10 — THE FINAL HERO SHOT

The camera continues tracking backward as she approaches slightly closer. Burning wreckage and towering flames remain visible behind her.

She walks steadily toward the lens, looking straight ahead with an intimidating, expressionless gaze.

End on a powerful cinematic shot of the woman perfectly centered against the enormous burning background.

SOUND DESIGN

Quiet nighttime ambience, soft barefoot footsteps against asphalt, subtle cigarette inhale, sudden explosive ignition, enormous deep bass explosion, roaring flames, rushing air, and falling debris.

STRICT REQUIREMENTS

One uninterrupted tracking shot. No cuts, no camera angle changes, no slow motion, no dialogue, no subtitles, no text overlays. The woman's face and clothing must remain perfectly consistent. The explosion must remain behind her, never engulfing her. Physically convincing fire, smoke, lighting, and debris. No cartoon effects, no artificial-looking CGI humans, no facial distortion, no duplicated limbs.
```

Create a 30-second ultra-realistic personal home-video of a young Korean woman going to a local gym for a simple workout. Use the attached image as the character reference and keep her face, hairstyle and overall appearance consistent throughout.

She leaves her home carrying a small gym bag and walks through the quiet neighborhood toward a nearby local gym. Before entering, she ties her long black hair into a neat ponytail and adjusts her gym bag.

Inside the gym, she changes into realistic workout clothes: a fitted black athletic crop top, high-waisted charcoal leggings, clean white training shoes, and a small fitness watch. She places her bag and water bottle beside a workout bench and begins stretching.

She starts with light dumbbell exercises, then does bodyweight squats, lunges and jumping-jack exercises. Show realistic movement, controlled breathing and natural effort. Her face gradually becomes slightly sweaty as the workout continues.

She pauses, grabs her water bottle and drinks several times, then wipes sweat from her forehead and neck with a small white towel. She looks at herself in the gym mirror, laughs at how sweaty she has become and smiles.

She continues with a short treadmill run, breathing naturally and occasionally laughing when she gets tired. She slows down, steps off carefully, grabs her towel and wipes her face again.

Near the end, she sits on the bench catching her breath, drinks water, smiles toward the camera and says, “That was a good workout!” She gives a small laugh, picks up her gym bag and walks out.

Camera: Raw early-2000s consumer DV-camera footage — handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, exposure shifts, soft digital detail, mild digital noise, natural motion blur, occasional awkward zooms and authentic home-video imperfections. Casual friend-filmed feeling, not a polished fitness commercial.

Audio: Natural gym ambience only — footsteps, treadmill motor, weights gently hitting the floor, breathing, water bottle opening, towel movement, distant conversations, occasional laughter and air-conditioning hum. No music, no narration, no subtitles.

Consistency & realism: Keep the same woman, face, hairstyle, body proportions, gym outfit, shoes, towel, water bottle and gym bag consistent. Realistic sweat, skin texture, hair movement, breathing, exercise form and clothing physics. Natural expressions and genuine laughter.

Negative: CGI look, plastic skin, beauty filters, exaggerated muscles, impossible exercise movements, distorted hands, extra fingers, duplicated people, identity drift, outfit changes, floating objects, teleportation, unrealistic sweat, subtitles, logos, watermark, polished commercial cinematography.

This is not the ending you think it is.

Seedance 2.5 on @FishCreativeHQ 

Prompt

Opening (dark, tense, 0–10s)
Dimly lit rustic wooden cabin interior, moody low-key lighting, shadows, cool color grade. Close-up of a handsome man in his late 20s/early 30s: messy medium-length brown hair, light stubble/beard, striking blue-green eyes, tears streaming down his face, raw anguish. He wears a dark charcoal oversized hoodie with metallic jewelry/charms on the chest. He holds a black pistol pointed directly toward camera (first-person feel from the woman’s perspective), hand trembling, face crumpled in grief.

Cut to medium shot: a beautiful young woman with long wavy blonde hair standing in the cabin kitchen (wooden shelves, metal pots, rustic counters). She wears a slightly unbuttoned cream silk blouse and flowing white skirt. Her expression flips to fierce rage — brows furrowed, teeth bared, intense blue eyes locked on him.
Back to the man: he keeps the gun raised, crying harder, then slowly lowers it, arms opening wide in surrender/offering himself, hoodie stretching as he stands exposed. Extreme close-up of his wet face, eyes squeezed shut, tears falling.
Transition & second half (warm, romantic, 11–26s)
Sudden smash-cut to golden-hour sunlight. The same couple tumbling into tall grass and wildflowers, embracing tightly, leaves and petals flying. Warm, sun-drenched color grade, lens flares, shallow depth of field, dreamy filmic look.
They roll laughing in a sunlit meadow filled with yellow wildflowers. Close-ups: her radiant smile, eyes crinkled, his hand gently cupping her cheek; his peaceful closed-eye smile as she leans over him. Soft golden light wrapping their faces.

They run hand-in-hand away from camera through rolling green hills at sunset, her white dress and his light blue shirt catching the wind. Wide tracking shot.
They sit together on a grassy hilltop overlooking valleys, his arm around her, watching a massive orange sun. He kisses the top of her head. Final wide shot: the couple as small silhouettes against a vast pink-orange dusk sky, hills stretching into the distance, wildflowers in the foreground.
Style notes
Cinematic 2.35:1 or 16:9, filmic grain, dramatic contrast in first half, warm anamorphic flares and soft bloom in second half. Emotional, intimate, high-production drama. Slow-motion on key emotional beats (tears, embrace, running). No dialogue, only ambient sound implied (breathing, wind, distant birds). Mood: heartbreak → fierce confrontation → tender memory/fantasy of love.
```

What if every beautiful moment disappears before we even realize it was happening?

Prompt

A cinematic fashion short film, ~20 seconds, 24fps, anamorphic widescreen, filmic grain, teal-and-warm color grade with slightly crushed blacks and lifted highlights. Mood is poetic, solitary, and slightly melancholic — time passing through a historic European city (Haussmann stone facades, colonnades, courtyards, waterfront, ornate interiors). Heavy use of motion blur, whip pans, low-angle tracking, shallow depth of field, and mixed slow/fast motion.
**Shot sequence / visual language**
- Opening whip-pan across a row of pale stone arches and columns, interiors glowing warm behind glass, strong horizontal motion blur.
- Low-angle medium of the woman new version.jpeg walking toward camera through a historic courtyard; face slightly soft/out of focus at first, green shirt filling the frame, stone palace architecture behind her.
- She @[Image 1](image_1) looks upward, profile against a pale sky and ornate upper stories; contemplative, wind in her hair.
- Cut to a flock of pigeons exploding off a blue-shuttered historic facade, heavy motion blur, birds streaking across the frame.
- She sits on a stone balustrade between tall columns, one black boot kicked up, plaid skirt fanned, looking directly at camera with a still, distant expression.
- Close low-angle as she leans her head on her shoulder against a large arched window; warm interior light in the glass.
- Fast blurred street shot: a cyan pedestrian light, traffic, reflections on glass.
- Close tracking of her legs and slouchy black boots walking on grey stone pavement, skirt swinging.
- Overexposed high-key medium of her walking toward camera in a bright square, buildings and flags washed almost white.
- Profile/three-quarter as she walks along a waterfront or bridge at golden hour, city buildings and water lights behind her.
- Interior: ornate stained-glass lunette with a helmeted warrior, zodiac/animal motifs, sun flare through the panes, hanging lantern.
- Motion-blurred spin/whip of her turning in front of a colonnaded palace facade.
- Low tracking of her standing on a stone pedestal/balustrade, only boots and skirt visible at first.
- Low-angle walking shot looking up at her against grand columns and windows.
- She sits on the dark sculpted rim of a fountain, knees drawn up, looking off-camera.
- Extreme close-up of her hand trailing fingers through fountain water, ripples and reflections.
- Tight frontal portrait against a dark carved wooden door with iron scrollwork; intense eye contact, almost still.
- Abstract whip-pan of stone and sky.
- Final aerial of a dense European city (Paris-like rooftops, a pedestrian bridge over a cut, autumn trees, traffic).
**Voiceover (calm, slightly distant female voice, sparse, over ambient city + soft score)**  
Everything is already leaving.
"The dark holds what daylight drops.
A step is a promise the ground won’t keep.
Time doesn’t wait. It only looks back."
**Technical / style keywords**  
Cinematic fashion film, editorial movement, motion blur, anamorphic flares, golden-hour mixed with overcast stone, shallow DOF, low angles, tracking and whip pans, film grain, teal-orange grade, contemplative pacing, no dialogue except VO, high-end commercial look (think luxury fashion campaign meets short art film).

<summary><b>プロンプト全文（54 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Seedance 2.5 via @openart_ai made this farm day look way too real 😯

Prompt 

Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video showing an ordinary summer afternoon in the life of a young Korean woman visiting a small traditional farm and helping with the animals. No reference image.

A naturally pretty beautiful Korean woman in her early 20s, realistic natural skin texture, minimal makeup, relaxed and cheerful personality. Long black hair tied in a loose low ponytail. She wears a loose white cotton T-shirt, light olive-green cargo pants, brown ankle boots, and a beige canvas crossbody bag. Keep the same face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and overall appearance throughout the entire video.

A small traditional countryside farm outside Seoul during a warm summer afternoon. Wooden fences, green fields, an old farmhouse, barn, hay bales, vegetable patches, chickens, cows, buckets, farming tools, trees and natural countryside surroundings. The farm feels ordinary and lived-in, not like a tourist attraction.

The camera is ALWAYS positioned IN FRONT OF THE GIRL, never behind her. A friend stands or walks in front of her, casually filming her with a handheld consumer DV camcorder/smartphone. Her front face must remain clearly visible during the important moments. The camera naturally moves backward while she walks toward it. Use imperfect handheld movement, small shakes, slightly uneven framing, autofocus hunting, occasional exposure changes, natural motion blur, soft digital detail, mild image noise, faded early-2000s home-video colors, and occasional accidental zooms.

Absolutely NO rear-view shots, NO filming from behind, NO drone, NO gimbal, NO cinematic tracking shots, NO polished commercial look, NO beauty filter. It should genuinely feel like a friend casually recorded this afternoon.

TIMELINE

0:00–0:05 — Arriving at the Farm

The girl walks toward the farm entrance while the friend walks backward in front of her filming. She smiles naturally and looks around at the animals and fields. She says casually in Korean:

“오늘 농장에 왔어요.”

Natural footsteps, birds, insects and distant farm sounds.

0:05–0:10 — Feeding the Cow

Still filmed from the front, the girl approaches a calm cow and holds out fresh vegetables. The cow gently eats from her hand. She laughs naturally and looks at the camera, then back at the cow.

0:10–0:15 — Helping With Vegetables

Hard cut. The camera remains in front of her as she walks through a vegetable patch and picks fresh vegetables. She holds them up toward the camera with a happy smile.

0:15–0:20 — Chickens

Hard cut. Several chickens suddenly walk across the path in front of her. The camera stays facing her from the front as she laughs and carefully steps around them. One chicken comes close to her boots.

0:20–0:25 — Farmhouse Break

She sits outside the farmhouse with a basket of freshly picked vegetables and eats a piece of fresh fruit. She looks relaxed and slightly tired from working. She smiles at the camera and says:

“생각보다 재밌어요.”

0:25–0:30 — Walking Away / Goodbye
She gets up and walks toward the camera, then stops near the farm entrance. The friend keeps filming from directly in front of her. She gives a small wave and says:

“다음에 또 올게요. 안녕!”

She walks past the camera as the recording ends naturally.

AUDIO:
Only authentic location sound: birds, chickens, cows, insects, footsteps, leaves, distant farm activity and natural conversation. No background music, no narration, no artificial sound effects.

FINAL LOOK:
Completely believable real-life home-video footage. Slightly imperfect, spontaneous, unpolished and human. Front-facing camera coverage throughout the story, with the girl's face clearly visible.
```

</details>

A cinematic 30-second tropical travel vlog montage featuring a beautiful 20-year-old East Asian woman with dark hair exploring Bali during a dreamy summer getaway. Shot like an authentic luxury travel diary with handheld camera movement, candid moments, soft golden-hour sunlight, dreamy 35mm film aesthetics, warm vintage color grading, shallow depth of field, natural skin texture, atmospheric lighting, and cinematic storytelling. Maintain the same woman throughout every scene: dark hair, youthful appearance, natural makeup, elegant summer outfits, relaxed happy expression. Format: 4K cinematic video, 24fps, 35mm film grain, realistic handheld camera, soft focus, warm color palette, travel documentary style.

Scene 1 (0-4s) — Arrival & Village Street: A warm Balinese morning. The woman strolls down a narrow street lined with frangipani trees and stone shrines, wearing a linen wrap dress, sunglasses pushed up into her hair. Motorbikes pass softly blurred in the background, incense smoke drifts from a doorway offering. Camera trails her from behind, then swings into a close-up as she glances back over her shoulder and laughs, saying softly, "Okay, I think I already love it here."

Scene 2 (4-8s) — Cliffside Beach Discovery: She descends stone steps onto a narrow cliffside beach, turquoise water crashing against limestone cliffs behind her. She walks barefoot at the tideline, dress hem lifted slightly, waves curling over her feet. Low-angle shots of her footprints filling with foam, sunlight scattering across the water, jagged cliffs framing the horizon.

Scene 3 (8-12s) — Rice Terrace & Jungle Moments: A worm's-eye view looking up through banana leaves and bamboo, sunlight cutting through in warm shafts with soft lens flares. Cut to a close-up of her standing at the edge of an emerald rice terrace, wind lifting loose strands of hair as she looks out, quietly murmuring, "It's so green it doesn't look real."

Scene 4 (12-16s) — Warung Cafe & Slow Living: She sits alone at a small open-air warung overlooking the jungle, sipping fresh coconut water through a paper straw. Sunlight filters through woven bamboo shades. Close-up shots of her hands wrapped around the coconut, condensation beading on the shell, her contented half-smile as she watches the trees sway.

Scene 5 (16-20s) — Ocean Adventure: She paddles a wooden longboard through a calm turquoise lagoon, sunlight sparkling across the surface. Camera circles her at water level, capturing gentle ripples and distant green cliffs. She loses balance slightly, laughs out loud, and calls toward the camera, "Don't film this part — actually, keep filming it."

Scene 6 (20-24s) — Night Market Exploration: A glowing Balinese night market strung with paper lanterns, satay smoke curling into the air, vendors calling out prices. She weaves through the crowd sampling grilled skewers and mango sticky rice, her face lit by warm string lights and passing motorbike headlights. Cinematic close-ups of her eyes widening at the taste, lanterns blurred into soft bokeh behind her.

Scene 7 (24-27s) — Golden Sunset Ending: A wide silhouette shot of her standing at the shoreline as the sun sinks into the sea, sky burning orange and violet, reflections rippling across the wet sand. Waves wash gently around her ankles as she tilts her face toward the last light, one hand shielding her eyes.

Scene 8 (27-30s) — Hotel Night Reflection: A rooftop pool bar overlooking a glittering coastal skyline at night. She leans against the railing in a simple white dress, curtain of hair moving in the breeze, city lights reflected in her eyes. Final intimate close-up of her curled up on the hotel bed, propped on one elbow, looking directly into the lens with a soft, warm smile as she says, "Goodnight from Bali."

Camera Style: Authentic travel vlog cinematography, handheld camera shake, smooth cinematic transitions, slow push-ins, natural drifting movement, occasional POV shots, realistic autofocus hunting, subtle motion blur.

Visual Style: Dreamy Bali vacation film, boutique travel-brand aesthetic, soft golden sunlight, realistic skin texture, cinematic shallow depth of field, nostalgic 35mm film look, warm atmospheric tones, natural unscripted expressions, emotional storytelling.

Avoid: cartoon style, CGI look, plastic skin, unrealistic face, inconsistent character appearance, changing hairstyle, extra fingers, distorted body, artificial lighting, oversaturated colors, blurry face, unnatural movements, duplicate people.

Realism that makes ordinary life feel special

Seedance 2.5 

Prompt

Create a 30-second, 16:9 ultra-realistic handheld lifestyle vlog following the young Japanese woman in her 20s through an ordinary day in Kyoto. Keep her face, identity, hairstyle, body, outfit, accessories, and proportions perfectly consistent.

She wears a fitted pastel yellow short top, straight-leg light denim jeans, yellow canvas sneakers, a small tan tote bag, and thin gold hoop earrings throughout.

Style: Genuine footage filmed casually by a close friend on a smartphone/compact camera. Handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus/exposure shifts, natural motion blur, mild digital noise, realistic skin texture, occasional late camera reactions. 0–5s: Quiet residential Kyoto street, narrow lane with wooden facades. She steps out of her apartment, glances at her phone, notices the camera, smiles softly, tucks her hair behind her ear and says naturally: "今日も一緒に行こう。"

5–11s: Enters a small neighborhood bakery/konbini-style shop, notices a fresh melon pan and says: "これ、いい匂いがする。" Buys it, takes a bite outside, and smiles at the camera.

11–17s: Boards a local city bus and sits by the window. Kyoto streets and old buildings pass outside with realistic reflections and sunlight. She quietly says: "こういう日は、歩きたくなるな。"

17–23s: Gets off near a canal-side walking path. Buys a drink from a small vending machine or cart. A cyclist passes unexpectedly close; she steps aside, laughs, and says: "今、ちょっとびっくりした。"

23–27s: Golden-hour canal-side scene. She sits on a low stone ledge or bench, places her drink beside her, removes one earbud, watches the water, and gives a small genuine smile as the wind moves her hair.

27–30s: She stands and walks away. After a few steps, looks back and says: "次は一緒に来てね。" She continues walking as the camera follows behind. Recording naturally ends around 29.5s.

REALISM: Accurate human biomechanics, grounded feet, continuous objects, realistic bus movement, natural walking/eating/sitting, consistent architecture and wardrobe. No teleportation, morphing, identity drift, duplicated/disappearing objects, distorted hands or limbs.

AUDIO: Natural Japanese dialogue with accurate lip-sync, phone-mic quality, footsteps, traffic, birds, bakery sounds, bus engine, bicycle bell, wind and city ambience. No music, narrator, or cinematic SFX.

Absolutely no subtitles, captions, on-screen text, logos, watermarks, or UI.

Made with Seedance 2.5 on @HighSignal_AI 

🎬 MAIN VIDEO PROMPT

Use the uploaded image as the exact visual reference. Create a highly detailed, cinematic 10-second magical transformation video. Keep the overall composition, character appearance, lighting, camera quality, and environment consistent with the reference.

⏱️ 0–2 seconds — Jar & Mud
The subject is holding a small transparent glass jar/container carefully in both hands. Inside the jar is thick, dark, moist magical mud/soil with realistic texture. The camera slowly pushes toward the jar, focusing on the mud. Tiny particles of soil begin to move naturally.

🌱 2–5 seconds — Mud Comes Alive
The mud inside the jar starts gently swirling and rising as if something is forming underneath it. Small cracks appear across the surface of the mud. Tiny pieces of soil lift into the air and fall naturally. A subtle magical glow begins to emerge from inside the mud, while keeping the scene photorealistic rather than cartoonish.

🐣 5–8 seconds — Bird Emerges
A small realistic bird slowly emerges from the mud inside the jar. First, its tiny head appears, followed by its neck, wings, and body. Wet mud naturally falls away from its feathers as it comes out. The bird gradually becomes fully formed, with extremely detailed feathers, realistic eyes, tiny beak, and natural movements. The emergence should feel magical but physically believable.

🐦 8–10 seconds — Final Moment
The bird completely emerges from the mud and gently spreads its wings. A few tiny pieces of mud fall back into the jar. The bird looks around naturally and gives a subtle wing movement. The camera finishes with a beautiful close-up of the bird sitting on/above the mud inside the jar, creating a mysterious and magical final shot.

🎥 CAMERA & VISUAL STYLE

Duration: 10 seconds
Format: 9:16 vertical
Ultra-photorealistic
Cinematic fantasy realism
Smooth slow camera movement

Would you take out your phone to make videos or run first?

Seedance 2.5 on @DomoAI_ 

prompt

LIVE-ACTION CINEMATIC REALISM. Every frame is photographed as real live-action footage: a real adult woman, real city, real pedestrians, real domestic pets, and a real enormous mascot plush statue. Real skin with pores, real wavy blonde hair with individual strands, real cotton, nylon, leather, metal, wet asphalt, glass, concrete and plush fibers. No illustration, painting, anime, CGI, 3D render, or artificial surface.

CHARACTER LOCK:

The same clearly adult street-fashion archer from @[Image 1](image_1) in every shot. Preserve her exact face, natural facial geometry, dark eyes, skin tone, expression, extremely long straight wavy blonde hair, thick blunt bangs, and royal-blue backward baseball cap with small decorative details but no readable text. She is 178 cm tall, XS size, very slim elongated runway-model proportions. Her outfit never changes: oversized white-to-light-grey graphic cotton T-shirt with a deep plunging neckline that that accentuates her large cleavage., faint doodle motifs but no readable lettering, dusty rose-pink wide cargo trousers with dimensional pockets and heavy folds, black belt with silver hardware, colorful keychain charms, layered bead necklaces, and red low-top sneakers with white soles.
ARCHERY GEAR:
She carries a long dark laminated-wood recurve bow with a leather-wrapped grip, visible taut string, realistic curved limbs and subtle wear. A dark-brown leather quiver crosses diagonally over her back, holding six to eight arrows with natural wood shafts and cream-white feather fletching. She wears matching brown leather archery gloves with stitched palms, reinforced knuckles and wrist straps, plus a fitted leather forearm bracer on the bow arm and a slim finger tab or draw glove. The archery equipment is integrated into her street-fashion styling, not a historical costume.
LOCATION:
A dense urban shopping district and pedestrian plaza at blue hour, wet asphalt reflecting storefront light, concrete towers, glass façades, crosswalk signals, street lamps, overhead transit lines, narrow side streets, urban haze, and a large original unbranded mascot plush statue installed in the center of the plaza. The mascot is enormous, approximately three stories tall, with a soft cream-and-pastel plush body, oversized rounded head, expressive embroidered eyes, visible seams, padded limbs and tactile fabric. No copyrighted character, logo or readable text.
STORY — 12 SHOTS, ONE CONTINUOUS EVENT:
1. 0–1.5s, LOW WIDE. Camera whips right past two foreground pedestrians and a street barrier. She bursts through the wet plaza at a low run, bow held low, red sneakers splashing lightly, dusty rose cargo fabric and extremely long hair streaming behind her.
2. 1.5–3s, EXTREME CLOSE, LOCKED. A commuter’s eyes appear above a scarf behind a transparent umbrella. The eyes snap toward the plaza as nearby city lights reflect across the wet plastic.
3. 3–4.5s, CLOSE, FAST PUSH-IN. Two pedestrians and their leashed pets in the distance suddenly turn toward the giant mascot statue. A small dog raises its ears while a cat inside a carrier shifts and looks outward.
4. 4.5–6s, TRACKING MEDIUM. Camera runs beside her through the moving crowd at shoulder height. People step aside, coats and shopping bags sway, her long black hair, oversized T-shirt and cargo trousers carry real momentum as she reaches toward the quiver.
5. 6–7.5s, LOW ANGLE CLOSE. Her red sneaker plants on the wet pavement. She straightens hard and raises the dark recurve bow into frame. The giant mascot statue is visible far behind her between buildings.
6. 7.5–9s, MEDIUM FROM HER LEFT. Camera orbits behind her as she draws an arrow from the quiver, nocks it and pulls the string to full draw. The bow limbs flex visibly; pedestrians freeze at a safe distance, watching.
7. 9–10.5s, MACRO, LOCKED, razor-shallow focus. Her leather-gloved fingers grip the string, stitched glove texture, finger tab and arrow shaft sharply resolved. Her eye, blue cap and city lights remain soft behind. The string creeps back the last centimetre. The crowd noise drops for one hard beat.
8. 10.5–12s, WIDE FROM BEHIND AND ABOVE THE ARROW, LOCKED. She releases. The string snaps and blurs. The arrow tears forward between streetlights and glass buildings toward the giant mascot statue.
9. 12–14s, MEDIUM CLOSE TRACKING THE ARROW. The arrow breaks apart in mid-air: its front half dissolves into millions of bright rose-pink glitter particles streaming behind it, each particle catching the city light separately while the feathered tail remains intact. The arrow has no point.
10. 14–16s, WIDE LOW ANGLE. The glitter stream strikes the plush mascot’s chest and races through its seams as a bright rose-pink wave. Plush fibers ripple outward from the impact point. Pink reflections pulse across the wet pavement and nearby windows.
11. 16–18s, WIDE LOW ANGLE, FAST PULLBACK. The enormous mascot plush statue activates and rises into a fully living presence. Its padded head lifts, embroidered eyes illuminate softly, oversized arms move, seams flex, and rose-pink glowing fabric appliqués spread across its plush surface from the impact point. It remains clearly soft, textile-based and physically heavy, not a CGI monster.
12. 18–20s, FINALE, WIDE FROM BEHIND. Camera rises behind her as she lowers the bow and lets her weight settle naturally onto one hip. She looks up at the awakened mascot with a small confident smile. Pedestrians gather and react, some stepping backward, others pointing or raising phones. Dogs tug lightly at their leashes, a small pet in a carrier shifts, and the giant mascot waves slowly above the plaza. Glitter continues to fall through the city light.
Vary the framing aggressively: extreme close-up, macro, close, medium, tracking, low wide and overhead. Never repeat the same shot type twice consecutively. Shots 2, 7 and 8 are locked; all others use clear camera movement. Every action must be instantly readable.
LOOK:
Photorealistic live-action large-format colour film with a refined three-strip Technicolor response. Concrete, glass, asphalt and distant architecture remain cool blue-grey and slightly desaturated. Saturated colours are the royal-blue cap, dusty rose cargo trousers, red sneakers, colorful keychain charms, rose-pink glitter, and the cream-and-pastel mascot plush. Fine photochemical grain, subtle halation on streetlights and glitter, realistic wet reflections, tactile cotton, nylon, leather, metal and plush fibers. Photographed reality, never drawn.
LIGHTING:
Blue-hour city light mixed with warm street lamps and storefront spill at approximately 4200K. Cooler sky fill at 4:1. Streetlights rim her long black hair, cap, bow and quiver. Thin urban mist and steam rise from vents and wet pavement. The glitter emits separate rose-pink specular points and casts soft pink reflections onto the mascot, asphalt, glass and her face.
CAUSE AND EFFECT:
Pedestrians and pets notice the strange event before the transformation, so their final reactions read as consequences. The glitter must visibly travel in sequence: arrow releases, arrow breaks apart, particles stream through the city, glitter strikes the mascot chest, light travels through the seams, the plush statue activates, then the crowd and pets react. Never cut directly to an already-animated mascot.
PHYSICS:
Real mass, real air and believable momentum. Bow limbs flex, string snaps, arrow flight has directional force, glitter behaves as millions of separate solid particles carried by fast air, and plush fabric compresses, ripples and rebounds with weight. Cargo fabric, T-shirt, hair, quiver strap and keychains respond to acceleration. Wet pavement reflects movement without becoming a mirror. The giant mascot moves slowly because of its padded mass. No slow motion; all shots run at real-time speed.
AUDIO:
Wet footsteps, light splashes, distant traffic, pedestrian chatter, shopping bags and coats moving, dog tags and leash tension, a faint pet sound, bowstring creak, sharp release snap, arrow hiss, crystalline glitter shimmer, plush fabric shifting, soft mechanical-like seam tension, crowd gasps and footsteps. No music.
ON-SCREEN TEXT:
None. No readable text, subtitles, numbers, captions, UI, logos, brand marks or watermark anywhere.
OUTPUT:
20 seconds, horizontal 16:9, 4K, continuous cinematic live-action realism. #DomoAi #DomoAiCPP
