THE FALLEN ANGEL

Seedance 2.5 on @ElevenLabs @ElevenCreative #ElevenLabsAmbassador

prompt

Female Lead: A beautiful 24-year-old young woman @Image 2, with pale fair skin, delicate facial features, striking blue eyes, and long wavy blonde hair partially hidden beneath a black hood. She wears a long black Victorian gothic-style hooded cloak over an elegant black dress. Around her neck hangs a silver cross pendant featuring a deep-red gemstone. Her expression is mysterious, frightened, and emotionally intense.

MAKEUP DETAILS:

Beautiful soft-glam makeup with warm, glowing olive-tan undertones while preserving her naturally fair complexion and realistic skin texture. Subtle golden highlights, softly sculpted cheekbones, defined sharp eyebrows, and beautifully enhanced eyes.

Soft warm-toned eyeshadow, delicate eyeliner, long curled lashes, and subtle shimmer on the eyelids. Light peachy-pink blush blended naturally across the cheeks, gentle bronzer for a sun-kissed glow, and luminous highlights on the cheekbones, nose bridge, and cupid's bow.

Full, plump lips with a rosy-pink to soft-red lip tint, finished with high-shine, wet-look glossy lip gloss.

Fresh, radiant, feminine beauty makeup with a naturally dewy finish. Keep the makeup realistic, elegant, and consistent across every shot, even under the dark gothic lighting.

Fallen Angel: A handsome yet terrifying supernatural male angel, approximately 28 years old, with extremely pale skin, short messy platinum-white hair, sharp facial features, and piercing eyes.

He wears a luxurious ivory-white medieval angelic outfit with intricate detailing. Two enormous black feathered wings extend from his back.

He carries an ancient gothic bow loaded with a supernatural arrow featuring a glowing blood-red crystal arrowhead.

His presence is intimidating, mysterious, and otherworldly.

SCENE 1 (0–3 Seconds) — The Haunted Graveyard

An ancient, abandoned European gothic cemetery stretches beneath a dark, overcast sky.

The environment is filled with weathered stone gravestones, broken crosses, angel statues, and enormous gothic church towers.

Thick grey fog spreads across the ground while dark green trees sway gently in the cold wind.

A young woman @Blonde-Influencer, wearing a flowing black gothic cloak, slowly walks through the center of the cemetery.

Suddenly, she stops and looks toward the sky.

A powerful gust of wind dramatically lifts and ripples her long black cloak.

Camera: Low-angle tracking shot following her movement, transitioning into a slow cinematic upward tilt.

SCENE 2 (3–6 Seconds) — The Fallen Angel Appears

Suddenly, a mysterious white-haired fallen angel descends from the dark sky.

His enormous black feathered wings spread fully outward, creating a terrifying silhouette against the cloudy atmosphere.

He hovers above a tall stone cross in the cemetery.

His luxurious white clothing flutters naturally in the strong wind while individual black feathers move realistically.

The young woman looks up at him in complete shock.

Behind the angel, an ancient gothic cathedral and fog-covered trees dominate the background.

Camera: Epic wide cinematic shot with a dramatic low-angle perspective, emphasizing the angel's supernatural presence and enormous wings.

SCENE 3 (6–9 Seconds) — The Blood Arrow

The fallen angel raises his ancient gothic bow.

The weapon is crafted from dark metallic materials, twisted branch-like structures, and glowing red gemstones embedded throughout its surface.

He aggressively pulls back the bowstring.

A supernatural arrow materializes, featuring a sharp blood-red crystal arrowhead radiating intense crimson energy.

His piercing eyes reveal cold determination.

He aims directly at the young woman @Image 2 standing below.

Without hesitation, he releases the bowstring.

The glowing arrow launches forward at tremendous speed, cutting violently through the cold cemetery air.

A faint red supernatural trail follows its trajectory.

Camera: Extreme close-up of the intricate gothic bow, dramatic side-angle shot of the angel releasing the arrow, followed by a high-speed tracking shot following the projectile.

SCENE 4 (9–12 Seconds) — The Terrifying Realization

The young woman stands motionless in the middle of the cemetery.

Her black hood frames her pale, beautifully made-up face.

Suddenly, her blue eyes widen in terror.

Her glossy lips part slightly as she realizes the supernatural arrow is flying directly toward her.

Her expression shifts from confusion to overwhelming fear and disbelief.

The cold wind moves loose strands of her blonde hair across her face.

Her breathing becomes shallow as the terrifying realization sinks in.

Camera: Tight cinematic facial close-up with subtle handheld movement, dramatic shallow depth of field, and realistic emotional microexpressions.

SCENE 5 (12–15 Seconds) — The Mysterious Figures

Behind the young woman stands an ancient gothic stone archway.

Inside the dark archway, three mysterious men dressed in black Victorian religious clothing stand silently.

Their faces are partially concealed by deep shadows.

None of them move.

They silently observe the young woman as though they have been expecting this supernatural event.

Thick fog slowly curls around the stone pillars and spreads across the ground.

The atmosphere becomes increasingly unsettling.

Camera: Over-the-shoulder shot from behind the young woman, followed by a slow cinematic zoom toward the three mysterious figures.

SCENE 6 (15–18 Seconds) — The Supernatural Awakening

Extreme close-up of the young woman's eyes.

A mysterious red supernatural reflection suddenly appears within her blue irises.

Her eyelids slowly close.

For a brief moment, everything becomes almost completely silent.

Then she opens her eyes again.

Her previous expression of terror has vanished.

Instead, her gaze becomes intensely focused, mysterious, and unnaturally powerful.

A subtle crimson glow reflects across her pupils, suggesting the awakening of an ancient supernatural force within her.

Her facial expression remains controlled, calm, and frighteningly intense.

Camera: Extreme macro close-up of her eyes, slow cinematic push-in, dramatic low-key lighting, realistic iris reflections, and detailed facial textures.

SCENE 7 (18–20 Seconds) — The Dark Revelation

The camera suddenly reveals a wide atmospheric view of the cemetery.

The young woman stands among ancient gravestones, surrounded by rapidly swirling fog.

Her long black cloak flies dramatically in the powerful supernatural wind.

The cemetery appears darker and more threatening than before.

A sharp cinematic cut reveals a handsome young priest @Image 1, dressed in a traditional black clerical outfit with a crisp white priest collar.

His expression is extremely serious, mysterious, and emotionally restrained.

An older man stands partially visible behind him, his face obscured by shadows.

The young priest stares directly ahead, as though he knows a terrifying secret about the fallen angel and the young woman.

His expression suggests that something far more dangerous is about to happen.

Camera: Wide atmospheric cemetery shot, followed by a sharp cinematic close-up of the priest's face.

End on a powerful dramatic bass impact and an abrupt cut to black.

SOUND DESIGN

Deep atmospheric supernatural horror music throughout the sequence.

Distant gothic church bells echoing through the cemetery.

Cold wind blowing through ancient gravestones and trees.

Heavy, realistic fluttering sounds from the fallen angel's enormous wings.
Deep supernatural low-frequency rumbling during the angel's descent.
Sharp metallic bowstring tension and release.
Extremely fast arrow whoosh with supernatural energy distortion.
Intense heartbeat sounds during the woman's terrifying realization.
Sudden near-total silence during her supernatural awakening.
Powerful cinematic bass impact during the final revelation.
STRICT VISUAL INSTRUCTIONS
Character Consistency: Maintain identical character identities, facial structures, hairstyles, eye colors, makeup, outfits, and accessories throughout the entire sequence. Preserve the female lead's exact reference-image identity.

Fallen Angel Wings: The fallen angel must always have exactly two enormous anatomically consistent black feathered wings. Wings must remain naturally attached to his back, with realistic feather movement, believable joints, and physically accurate motion.

Environment Consistency: Maintain the same gothic cemetery architecture, ancient cathedral, gravestones, stone crosses, fog distribution, and atmospheric lighting across all scenes.

Realistic Physics: Use realistic cloth simulation, believable body movement, natural facial expressions, accurate wing motion, and physically consistent arrow trajectories.

Cinematic Quality: Hollywood-level visual effects, photorealistic skin textures, realistic environmental details, volumetric fog, dramatic shadows, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, and sophisticated dark fantasy color grading.

Avoid: Cartoon physics, distorted faces, inconsistent makeup, extra limbs, duplicated wings, unnatural anatomy, random character changes, flickering costumes, unrealistic movements, inconsistent environments, and excessive artificial-looking visual effects.

Overall Mood: Mysterious, haunting, supernatural, gothic, emotionally intense, visually spectacular, and deeply unsettling, with the atmosphere of a premium Hollywood dark fantasy movie trailer.

```
AIRPORT ZOMBIE ESCAPE — THE LAST FLIGHT

Duration: 20 seconds
Aspect Ratio: 16:9

Style: Ultra-photorealistic live-action zombie survival action film, cinematic Hollywood production.

1. CHARACTER CONSISTENCY

Female Lead: A young adult blonde schoolgirl with long light-blonde hair, wearing a navy-blue school blazer with white trim, a white collared shirt, a large dark red ribbon bow, a dark red-and-navy plaid pleated skirt, white knee-high socks, and black school shoes. She has a frightened but determined expression. Her hair moves naturally as she runs and jumps. Preserve her exact face, hairstyle, school uniform, and body proportions throughout the video.

Zombies: Several terrifying adult human zombies with pale dirty skin, messy dark hair, damaged faces, and worn brown and gray clothing. They run aggressively after the girl with their arms reaching forward. Preserve their realistic human proportions and horror makeup.

Military Personnel: Male military crew members wearing dark navy military uniforms with visible yellow insignia and patches. They are positioned inside the open rear cargo entrance of the aircraft and reach out to help the girl climb aboard.

2. LOCATION AND ENVIRONMENT

The sequence begins inside a large modern international airport terminal. The terminal features a high glass ceiling, large floor-to-ceiling windows, polished gray flooring, blue airport seating, luggage trolleys, glass partitions, and a large open atrium with a visible lower level.

The sequence then moves outside through automatic sliding glass doors onto an airport runway and aircraft service area. A large gray military cargo aircraft is parked with its rear cargo ramp fully lowered. The aircraft interior is dark, illuminated by rows of overhead lights.

Maintain realistic airport architecture, natural daylight, runway markings, aircraft positioning, and consistent character appearances.

3. SHOT-BY-SHOT VIDEO SEQUENCE

SHOT 1 — 0–2 SECONDS: RUNNING FROM THE ZOMBIES

Begin with a frontal tracking shot of the blonde schoolgirl running directly toward the camera through a modern airport terminal. Her navy blazer moves with her running motion, her blonde hair flows behind her, and her expression is frightened.

A large group of zombies is chasing her from behind across the airport terminal. The glass ceiling, blue seating, luggage carts, and polished floor remain visible. The camera moves backward smoothly as she runs toward it.

SHOT 2 — 2–4 SECONDS: THE ZOMBIES GET CLOSER

Continue the frontal running shot. The girl runs faster while several zombies close the distance behind her. One zombie comes dangerously close to her shoulder and reaches toward her from behind.

She keeps running without stopping. Use a dynamic tracking camera, natural motion blur, and realistic foot contact with the airport floor.

SHOT 3 — 4–6 SECONDS: APPROACHING THE LARGE OPENING

The girl runs toward a large open atrium area inside the terminal. The floor ahead reveals a wide opening overlooking the lower airport level. A zombie follows closely behind her.

Use a wide side-angle shot to reveal the large architectural gap, the lower floor, glass railings, and the height of the terminal. She prepares to jump across the opening.

SHOT 4 — 6–8 SECONDS: THE BIG JUMP

Capture the girl performing a powerful running jump across the large opening in the airport floor. Her body becomes fully airborne, both legs extended, arms raised for balance, and blonde hair flowing behind her.

A zombie lunges toward her from the opposite side but fails to catch her. Use a wide cinematic side view showing the full jump and the open space below.

SHOT 5 — 8–10 SECONDS: LANDING AND FALLING

The girl reaches the other side but loses her balance during the landing. She falls onto the polished terminal floor and slides forward, ending in a seated or low position.

Her hands touch the floor as she tries to stop herself. The zombie remains behind her in the distance. Use a medium-wide shot with realistic momentum, body weight, and floor contact.

SHOT 6 — 10–12 SECONDS: ESCAPING THROUGH THE AIRPORT EXIT

Cut to the exterior-facing airport entrance. The automatic glass sliding doors open as the girl runs through them toward the outside.

She moves from the bright airport terminal onto the airport service area. Keep the glass entrance and building facade visible behind her. The camera follows her from the front as she exits.

SHOT 7 — 12–15 SECONDS: ZOMBIES ON THE RUNWAY

Cut to a wide outdoor tracking shot. The girl runs along the airport service road with a large gray military cargo aircraft visible ahead.

Several zombies chase her across the runway behind her. The zombies move aggressively, reaching toward her as she runs. The aircraft and its large rear cargo opening become increasingly visible.

Use realistic airport daylight, runway markings, and cinematic motion blur.

SHOT 8 — 15–18 SECONDS: REACHING THE CARGO AIRCRAFT

The girl approaches the large military cargo aircraft. Its rear cargo ramp is lowered, creating an open entrance into the aircraft.

She runs directly toward the ramp, looking upward at the people inside. Several military crew members in dark navy uniforms stand inside the illuminated cargo compartment. One crew member leans forward and extends his hand toward her.

The zombies continue chasing her from behind. Use a low-angle shot that emphasizes the aircraft's size and the urgency of her escape.

SHOT 9 — 18–20 SECONDS: CLIMBING INTO SAFETY

Cut to a low-angle close shot from behind the girl as she reaches the lowered aircraft ramp. She places both hands on the ramp edge and begins climbing upward into the cargo compartment.

A male military crew member in a dark navy uniform reaches down and grabs her hand or forearm to help pull her inside. Her white socks, black shoes, plaid skirt, and navy blazer remain clearly consistent.

The zombies are still visible running across the runway below and behind her. End with the girl being pulled upward toward the illuminated aircraft interior.

4. CAMERA AND CINEMATOGRAPHY

Use realistic live-action action cinematography:

- Frontal backward-tracking camera during the airport chase.
- Wide architectural shots showing the airport atrium and open floor gap.
- Side-angle slow-motion emphasis during the jump.
- Medium-wide shot during the landing and fall.
- Smooth tracking through the automatic glass doors.
- Wide runway shot showing the military aircraft and chasing zombies.
- Dramatic low-angle view of the aircraft cargo ramp.
- Close-up of the military crew member reaching down to help the girl.

Maintain accurate spatial continuity between the terminal, glass exit, runway, and aircraft.

5. AUDIO DESIGN

- Airport terminal ambience and distant announcements.
- Rapid footsteps on polished flooring.
- Zombie growls, screams, and heavy running footsteps.
- Clothing movement and the girl's frightened breathing.
- A dramatic whoosh during the jump.
- Realistic sliding impact during her landing.
- Automatic glass door opening sounds.
- Wind and distant aircraft engine ambience outside.
- Military aircraft interior mechanical hum.
- Metal cargo ramp sounds and footsteps.
- A brief urgent sound as the military crew member grabs her hand.

No unnecessary narration. Preserve the tense zombie survival atmosphere.

6. STRICT NEGATIVE PROMPT

No ATM, no bank, no school classroom, no rooftop, no underground warehouse, no survivor shelter, no bathroom, no purple energy, no blue magical powers, no fighting choreography, no guns in the girl's hands, no changing blonde hair, no changing school uniform, no different female face, no duplicated girl, no extra limbs, no distorted hands, no changing airport architecture, no changing aircraft type, no helicopter, no random vehicles, no cartoon, no anime, no excessive gore, no daytime-to-nighttime transition, no random subtitles, no watermark.

FINAL OUTPUT

Create one coherent 20-second photorealistic zombie survival action sequence matching the reference video in exact story progression: a blonde schoolgirl in a navy school uniform runs through a modern airport while a large zombie horde chases her, jumps across a huge opening inside the terminal, loses balance and falls, escapes through the automatic glass doors, runs across the airport runway toward a large military cargo aircraft, and climbs its lowered rear ramp as military crew members reach down to pull her aboard while zombies continue chasing her.

```
MEET ME UNDER THE MAPLE TREE

A beautiful Korean love story, a deep-red scarf, and a precious memory that lives on through the years. ✨ From a heartwarming autumn romance in 1998 to a nostalgic moment in the present, this animated cinematic story brings timeless love to life with Seedance 2.5 on @openart_ai.

What memory would you keep forever? 

Prompt

Duration: 20 seconds | Cinematic 3D Animation | Korean Romance | Autumn Nostalgia | 16:9
ANIMATION STYLE AND VISUAL DIRECTION
Create a stunning, high-quality cinematic 3D animated short film set in South Korea. Use sophisticated character animation, beautifully detailed environments, realistic fabric textures, expressive faces, delicate lighting, and fluid natural movements.
The visual style should combine the charm of a beautifully animated romantic film with the emotional depth and cinematic composition of a nostalgic Korean love story.
Characters should have elegant, attractive facial features, expressive eyes, beautifully animated hair, and subtly stylized proportions while maintaining believable anatomy. Skin, knitted fabric, wood, falling leaves, and lighting should have rich, detailed textures.

Use warm autumn colors, golden sunlight, burgundy-red accents, soft atmospheric depth, delicate depth of field, and subtle film grain.

Important: This is a fully animated 3D film, not live-action. Avoid flat 2D animation, exaggerated cartoon proportions, childish character designs, anime styling, or plastic-looking 3D characters.

CHARACTER DESIGN AND CONTINUITY

THE ELDERLY WOMAN — PRESENT DAY

A graceful, exceptionally beautiful 72-year-old Korean woman with delicate facial features, expressive dark eyes, elegant silver-black hair tied loosely at the back, and naturally detailed age lines.

Her face should communicate kindness, wisdom, warmth, and a lifetime of beautiful memories. Her wrinkles should be delicately rendered rather than exaggerated. Give her a gentle smile and expressive eyes that can communicate happiness and nostalgia without dialogue.

She wears a vintage cream knitted cardigan over a muted-brown dress.

Her home is an old, cozy Korean wooden house featuring traditional wooden windows, faded curtains, aged furniture, a small wooden cabinet, an old analogue clock, and warm afternoon sunlight streaming inside.

On a wooden table sits an old keepsake box containing a faded photograph and a carefully preserved deep-red knitted scarf.

THE YOUNG WOMAN — 1998 FLASHBACK

The exact same character at 23 years old.

She is exceptionally beautiful, with delicate Korean facial features, large expressive dark eyes, smooth skin, long glossy black hair with soft bangs, and a naturally charming smile.

Her character design should feel elegant, romantic, and timeless, with subtle stylization, detailed hair strands, expressive facial animation, and graceful movements.

She wears a vintage cream wool coat, an ivory blouse, a dark pleated skirt, and a beautiful deep-red knitted scarf given to her by her first love.

The scarf must have detailed wool fibers, visible knitted patterns, soft folds, and natural movement.

THE BOYFRIEND — 1998 FLASHBACK

An exceptionally handsome 25-year-old Korean man with expressive dark eyes, a naturally defined jawline, thick black hair styled in a classic late-1990s Korean fashion, and a warm, sincere smile.

He wears a charcoal wool overcoat, a cream knitted sweater, dark trousers, and vintage leather shoes.

His animated expressions should be subtle and believable. His smile should feel shy, affectionate, and genuine.

Both characters must remain visually consistent throughout the flashback, with matching facial features, hairstyles, clothing, and proportions.

SCENE 1 — THE KEEPSAKE

TIME: 0–5 SECONDS

Inside a cozy old Korean wooden house, golden autumn sunlight streams through faded curtains.

Outside the window, red, amber, and golden maple leaves drift gently through the air.

The elderly woman sits beside a wooden table and slowly opens an old wooden keepsake box.

Inside is a faded photograph of her younger self standing beside a handsome young Korean man beneath a maple tree.

A deep-red knitted scarf rests carefully beneath the photograph.

She gently lifts the scarf with both hands. Her fingers softly move across its knitted texture.

A small, affectionate smile appears on her face. Her eyes glisten with a subtle tear as she remembers her first love.

Her expression is peaceful, happy, and filled with gratitude.

CAMERA: Slow cinematic push-in, beginning with a medium shot of the woman before moving into detailed close-ups of her hands, the red scarf, and the faded photograph.

ANIMATION DETAILS: Subtle hand movements, natural blinking, gentle breathing, delicate facial expressions, and softly moving curtains.

LIGHTING: Warm amber sunlight, soft shadows, beautiful highlights on the knitted wool, and realistic light bouncing off the wooden furniture.

SCENE 2 — A HAPPY MEMORY

TIME: 5–10 SECONDS

The camera slowly moves toward the old photograph.

The photograph fills the screen, and its image gradually transforms into a living, animated memory.

Suddenly, we are transported to autumn 1998.

A beautiful young Korean woman stands beneath a magnificent maple tree in a peaceful university garden. Golden leaves float around her as a gentle breeze moves her long black hair.

She wears her cream wool coat and the deep-red knitted scarf.

Her exceptionally handsome boyfriend approaches, carrying a small paper bag from an old-fashioned Korean bakery.

He smiles shyly and gently adjusts the scarf around her neck.

She looks up at him and laughs softly.

He brushes a loose strand of hair away from her face. They exchange a warm, affectionate smile.

Their expressions should communicate the excitement and tenderness of young love.

VISUAL EFFECT: A dreamy, softly glowing atmosphere with delicate floating leaves, gentle sunlight filtering through the maple canopy, subtle depth of field, and beautifully rendered autumn colors.

The memory should feel warm, happy, and precious, as though the elderly woman is reliving her favorite moment.

CAMERA: Gentle tracking movement, intimate close-ups, and graceful transitions between their expressions.

SCENE 3 — THEIR LITTLE WORLD

TIME: 10–15 SECONDS

The young couple walks along an old stone pathway covered in colorful fallen maple leaves.

The young man playfully steps on a dry leaf, making it crackle.

The woman laughs and lightly taps his arm.

He reaches for her hand. Their fingers gently interlock.

She looks at him with a playful smile before resting her head briefly against his shoulder.

They stop beneath the magnificent maple tree.

He gently adjusts the deep-red scarf around her neck and looks into her eyes.

BOYFRIEND — NATURAL KOREAN DIALOGUE:

"너한테 잘 어울려."

ENGLISH SUBTITLE:

"It looks beautiful on you."

The woman smiles shyly, lowers her eyes, and then looks back at him.

GIRL — NATURAL KOREAN DIALOGUE:

"정말?"

ENGLISH SUBTITLE:

"Really?"

He nods and smiles warmly.

She laughs softly.

A few maple leaves float between them as they continue looking at each other.

ANIMATION DETAILS: Fluid body movement, natural eye contact, subtle smiles, gentle hair movement, realistic scarf physics, and leaves responding naturally to the breeze.

The scene should feel intimate, playful, innocent, and genuinely happy.

SCENE 4 — THE MEMORY SHE KEPT

TIME: 15–20 SECONDS

A close-up of the young woman's joyful smile.

The image softly dissolves back into the present day.

The elderly woman sits beside the window in her wooden home, holding the same deep-red scarf against her chest.

She smiles tenderly at the photograph.

Her eyes glisten with nostalgic tears, but she does not look heartbroken. Instead, she looks peaceful and grateful to have experienced such beautiful love.

She carefully places the scarf beside the photograph and gently smooths its folded edge.

Outside the window, a golden maple leaf floats past.

She looks toward the autumn trees and whispers in Korean:

"행복했어… 정말."

ENGLISH SUBTITLE:

"I was happy… truly."

FINAL SHOT

A beautifully composed close-up of the faded photograph beside the preserved red scarf.

Warm sunlight falls across the wooden table. Tiny dust particles drift through the light.

The camera slowly pulls backward, revealing the peaceful room as golden leaves move beyond the window.

Fade to black.

FINAL TEXT:

"Some memories never grow old."

The text appears elegantly in the center of the screen, accompanied by a final gentle piano note.

CINEMATIC ANIMATION AND LIGHTING

High-end cinematic 3D animation with beautifully detailed character models, expressive facial animation, natural movement, realistic cloth simulation, detailed knitted fabric, individual hair strands, atmospheric lighting, and richly textured environments.

Use a warm autumn palette of amber, muted gold, burgundy, cream, and soft brown.

The present-day scenes should feel intimate, peaceful, and softly illuminated.

The flashback should feel brighter, warmer, and slightly dreamy, with delicate glowing sunlight and floating maple leaves.

Use smooth camera movement, cinematic composition, subtle depth of field, soft volumetric lighting, and gentle transitions.

Maintain consistent character identities and preserve the exact same deep-red scarf throughout the entire film.

AUDIO AND SUBTITLES

Soft nostalgic piano blended with gentle acoustic guitar.

Include delicate autumn ambience, rustling leaves, quiet footsteps, and subtle knitted-fabric sounds.

The Korean dialogue must sound natural, softly spoken, and emotionally authentic.

Display accurate English subtitles synchronized with the Korean dialogue. Use small, elegant white text with a subtle dark outline at the bottom of the frame.

No English voice-over.

NEGATIVE PROMPT

Flat 2D animation, childish cartoon style, exaggerated anime eyes, chibi characters, overly exaggerated facial expressions, plastic-looking skin, uncanny faces, distorted anatomy, inconsistent character designs, changing hairstyles, changing clothing, changing scarf color, unrealistic scarf physics, poorly animated hands, extra fingers, stiff movements, unnatural walking, excessive crying, horror atmosphere, dark depressing lighting, oversaturated colors, excessive blur, modern objects in the 1998 flashback, incorrect Korean subtitles, random text, logos, and watermarks.

FINAL OUTPUT

20-second cinematic 3D animated short film, 16:9 aspect ratio, 24 fps, high-quality rendering, beautiful original Korean character designs, detailed environments, smooth character animation, realistic cloth simulation, warm autumn lighting, seamless flashback transitions, expressive storytelling, and a deeply nostalgic yet happy romantic atmosphere.
```

Made with seedance 2.5

Prompt 👇 
Create a 20-second ultra-realistic, adorable 3D animated educational video for toddlers. A cute baby girl with big sparkling brown eyes, rosy cheeks, soft brown hair, and a white colorful kids' T-shirt sits at a small table in a beautiful preschool classroom. The classroom features pastel-colored walls, rainbow decorations, clouds, stars, colorful toys, pencils, and soft natural sunlight coming through the window.

Scene 1 – Banana 🍌: A hand gently presents a fresh yellow banana to the baby. She looks at it with curiosity and smiles happily. Display the word “BANANA” in large, colorful, playful 3D letters at the top of the screen, with a banana emoji.

Scene 2 – Apple 🍎: The hand presents a shiny red apple. The baby opens her mouth in excitement and reaches toward the fruit. Display “APPLE” in bright, multicolored 3D letters.

Scene 3 – Orange 🍊: A fresh orange is presented. The baby makes a cute surprised expression with her lips forming an O. Display “ORANGE” in bold, colorful letters.

Scene 4 – Grapes 🍇: Show a bunch of fresh purple grapes on the table. The baby looks down at the grapes and laughs joyfully. Display “GRAPES” in large purple 3D letters.

Scene 5 – Watermelon 🍉: A hand presents a juicy red watermelon slice with black seeds and green rind. The baby smiles excitedly and points toward it. Display “WATERMELON” in bright, playful letters.

Scene 6 – Mango 🥭: Present a ripe golden-yellow mango. The baby opens her mouth with excitement and smiles at the fruit. Display “MANGO” in colorful 3D letters.

Scene 7 – Strawberry 🍓: Show a fresh, bright red strawberry with green leaves. The baby raises her finger happily and smiles. Display “STRAWBERRY” in large, attractive letters.

Scene 8 – Pineapple 🍍: Present a beautiful golden pineapple with green spiky leaves. The baby claps her hands with delight. Display “PINEAPPLE” in colorful 3D letters.

Final Scene: The baby looks directly at the camera, smiles warmly, and gives a cute thumbs-up gesture.

🎨 Visual Style & Animation

Ultra-cute, high-quality 3D animation with a Pixar-inspired aesthetic.

Soft, realistic lighting and vibrant pastel colors.

Adorable facial expressions, natural blinking, and smooth hand movements.

Keep the same baby character, outfit, classroom, and camera angle throughout.

Each fruit must be clearly visible and recognizable.

Display each fruit's English name in large, bold, colorful 3D letters at the top.

Gentle camera movements, smooth transitions, and cheerful children's background music.

Add a friendly female voice pronouncing each fruit's English name clearly.

Vertical 9:16 format, high resolution, suitable for YouTube Shorts and children's educational content.

No scary elements, no distorted faces, no extra fingers, and no character changes.
```

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（90 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 60-second realistic day-in-the-life gym vlog following the same young Korean woman in her early twenties from waking up at home, getting ready, going to the gym, working out, and returning home.

Overall visual style

One continuous handheld front-facing smartphone selfie vlog perspective. She always films herself with the phone in her own hand at arm’s length. No third-person camera, tripod, cinematic camera movements, drones, or external filming.

Make everything look like authentic modern smartphone footage:

- Natural handheld shake and small framing imperfections
- Realistic walking movement and occasional autofocus hunting
- Minor exposure changes
- Natural front-camera lens distortion
- Soft natural morning light indoors and outdoors
- Realistic, unfiltered smartphone colors
- No beauty filter, skin smoothing, or cinematic color grading
- Same face and hair throughout the entire vlog
- Natural expressions, movements, and body language
- No subtitles, captions, logos, watermarks, or on-screen text
- No background music; use only natural diegetic sound

0–10 sec — Waking up

She wakes up in her cozy bedroom wearing a cute, loose casual night suit. She grabs her phone from beside the bed and immediately starts filming herself selfie-style. She looks sleepy, messy-haired, and slightly tired, gives a small yawn, stretches, and smiles at the camera.

She quietly says:

“Good morning… I really don’t feel like going to the gym today.”

She gets out of bed and walks toward the bathroom while holding the phone herself.

10–18 sec — Brushing teeth

In the bathroom, she films herself in the mirror/selfie view while brushing her teeth. Keep it casual and imperfect, with realistic bathroom ambience and running-water sounds.

She briefly looks into the camera with a sleepy expression, then rinses her mouth and smiles.

She says:

“But I promised myself I’d go, so… let’s do it.”

18–27 sec — Getting ready

Quick natural transition back to her bedroom. She changes from her night suit into a stylish but realistic gym outfit: fitted athletic top, high-waisted leggings or comfortable athletic bottoms, clean sneakers, and minimal accessories.

Show short selfie moments of her fixing her hair, adjusting her gym outfit, grabbing a water bottle and gym bag.

She looks into the phone and says:

“Okay, now I actually look ready.”

27–35 sec — Going to the gym

She leaves home with the phone in her hand. Film brief selfie moments while walking outside and naturally bouncing with her steps. Show quick glimpses of the street, morning traffic, buildings, and her gym bag.

She arrives at the gym and turns the phone slightly to show the entrance before bringing it back to her face.

Natural street and gym ambience only.

35–52 sec — Workout

Inside the gym, she starts with a short warm-up on a treadmill, then moves through a few exercises such as light strength training, squats, dumbbell exercises, and stretching.

Keep the camera mostly front-facing and handheld, occasionally tilting naturally toward the equipment before returning to her face.

She becomes slightly sweaty and out of breath but stays cheerful. She laughs and says:

“Okay… this is harder than I remembered.”

She takes a quick drink of water, catches her breath, then gives the camera a satisfied smile and continues her workout.

Include realistic sounds of footsteps, treadmill movement, weights clanking, machines, distant conversations, and gym room tone.

52–60 sec — Returning home

After finishing her workout, she leaves the gym and walks home. She looks pleasantly exhausted but happy.

Cut naturally to her back at home, still holding the phone. She drops her gym bag down, takes a drink of water, relaxes on the couch or bed, and looks into the camera with a tired smile.

She gives a small wave and says:

“Workout done. I’m exhausted… but I’m glad I went.”

She smiles, gives one final sleepy wave, and lowers the phone for a natural ending.

Final requirements

Keep the entire vlog fast, spontaneous, cozy, and realistic, with natural transitions between locations. The woman must remain visually consistent throughout: same facial identity, same hair, and realistic appearance. Her outfit changes only once, from the casual night suit to the gym outfit.

All dialogue must be naturally spoken with visible lip movement. Use only diegetic audio—bedroom ambience, bathroom sounds, footsteps, street noise, gym sounds, water bottle sounds, and home ambience. No music, captions, subtitles, logos, watermarks, or cinematic effects.
#Flovaai #Flovacpp
```

```
Cozy Sunrise Breakfast Vlog on @makeugc 

Seedance 2.5

Prompt

Character= Main Subject. Preserve her exact face, identity, hairstyle, skin tone, body proportions, and reference outfit throughout. Authentic Southeast Asian/Korean woman. No changes in appearance between shots.

Scene: A cozy apartment kitchen at sunrise, wooden countertops, small window overlooking a quiet neighborhood, ceramic mugs, fresh fruit, bread, eggs, and a little potted plant. Warm golden sunlight enters through the window. Peaceful, intimate morning atmosphere.

Style: RAW HANDHELD REALISM, early-2000s home-video aesthetic, casual camera sway, micro-shakes, frequent reframing, natural autofocus hunting, imperfect framing, occasional motion blur, slightly soft image quality, realistic skin texture, visible pores, natural facial expressions. Candid, spontaneous, unposed feeling. Camera operated casually by a friend, with occasional direct-to-camera interaction.

Timeline & Dialogues

00:00–00:04 — Sleepy Morning
She enters the kitchen, yawning slightly, rubbing her eyes, and opening the curtains. Golden sunlight fills the room.

Dialogue (sleepy, softly laughing): "Good morning... I seriously need coffee."
00:04–00:08 — Making Coffee
She scoops coffee into a mug, pours hot water, and stirs slowly. The camera moves closer to capture the steam.
Dialogue (playful): "Okay, first things first. Coffee before anything else."

00:08–00:12 — Breakfast Preparation
She cracks an egg into a frying pan, accidentally drops a tiny piece of shell, and laughs while removing it.
Dialogue (laughing naturally): "Oh my God... I'm such a professional chef."

00:12–00:16 — Toast Disaster
She checks her toast, realizes one side is slightly burnt, and holds it toward the camera with a disappointed expression.

Dialogue (sarcastically): "Wow. Five-star breakfast, everyone."
00:16–00:21 — Preparing the Plate
She arranges scrambled eggs, toast, and sliced strawberries on a small ceramic plate. She steals a strawberry and eats it while glancing mischievously at the camera.
Dialogue (cheerful): "Actually... this looks pretty good!"

00:21–00:25 — First Bite

She sits beside the window, takes a bite of toast, chews naturally, and nods with a satisfied smile. The handheld camera drifts slightly out of focus before recovering.
Dialogue (after swallowing): "Mmm. Okay, that's better than I expected."

00:25–00:30 — Funny Ending

She lifts her coffee mug toward the camera, takes a sip, then immediately reacts because it's too hot. She laughs and gently puts the mug down.
Dialogue (laughing): "Ah! Too hot! Okay... good morning to me."

The camera shakes slightly as the person filming laughs. Natural abrupt cut.

Camera & Audio
Camera: Handheld consumer MiniDV camcorder, imperfect zooms, spontaneous close-ups, occasional cropped headroom, natural exposure fluctuations, authentic home-video texture. No cinematic camera movement, no beauty filters, no artificial skin smoothing.

Audio: Natural kitchen ambience, birds outside, frying sounds, ceramic dishes, coffee stirring, soft footsteps, natural breathing, and clearly audible conversational dialogue. No background music, subtitles, or voiceover.
Dialogue delivery: Casual American-accented English, spontaneous delivery, natural pauses, little laughs, subtle changes in tone, realistic lip synchronization. She speaks only during the assigned moments, with enough time for each sentence.

Output: 30 seconds, 16:9 landscape, photorealistic, continuous character and outfit consistency, natural human motion, realistic hand-object interactions, believable everyday atmosphere.
```

Cinematic horror-action short film, opens with an instant attack, no slow build, grounded practical-effects style, maximum dynamic camera work. Setting: a quiet suburban street at dusk, a small group of neighbors gathered outside their homes. Muted authentic color grading, natural diegetic sound throughout, tension score entering immediately.
>
> **[0-1s]** Hard cut: a man stumbles out from between two houses, collapsing onto the street, convulsing violently.
>
> **[1-2s]** Nearby neighbors rush toward him to help, unaware of the danger.
>
> **[2-3s]** He suddenly lunges up, grabbing the nearest person by the arm.
>
> **[3-4s]** She screams, pulling back as his grip holds firm, veins darkening rapidly across his skin.
>
> **[4-5s]** He pulls her down, the group scattering in fear and confusion.
>
> **[5-6s]** She convulses hard on the pavement, veins spreading fast across her neck.
>
> **[6-7s]** She rises seconds later, eyes clouded, joining him, both turning toward the others.
>
> **[7-8s]** Loud cries of fear erupt through the street as neighbors scramble for their homes.
>
> **[8-9s]** The two infected move fast, catching a third neighbor near his front gate.
>
> **[9-10s]** He goes down, convulsing, rising moments later as one of them.
>
> **[10-11s]** Wide shot: panic spreads further down the street, people slamming doors and windows shut.
>
> **[11-12s]** A man grabs a baseball bat from his garage, stepping out cautiously.
>
> **[12-13s]** The three infected turn toward him, moving fast across the street.
>
> **[13-14s]** He swings hard at the first, a solid impact knocking it back into a parked car.
>
> **[14-15s]** A neighbor joins him, grabbing a shovel from a nearby yard.
>
> **[15-16s]** SLOW MOTION insert: she swings it into the second infected, striking it down.
>
> **[16-17s]** The third lunges at the man with the bat; he sidesteps and strikes again.
>
> **[17-18s]** It staggers, falling against a mailbox, still trying to rise.
>
> **[18-19s]** More neighbors emerge cautiously, grabbing garden tools and makeshift weapons.
>
> **[19-20s]** They surround the remaining infected, striking together in a coordinated effort.
>
> **[20-21s]** SLOW MOTION: the final blow connects, all three collapsing motionless on the street.
>
> **[21-23s]** Wide shot: the group stands over them, breathing hard, weapons still raised.
>
> **[23-25s]** Cries of fear slowly settle into exhausted relief among the neighbors.
>
> **[25-27s]** The man with the bat lowers it slowly, scanning the quiet street for any more movement.
>
> **[27-29s]** Neighbors check on each other, some embracing, others sitting down on the curb in shock.
>
> **[29-30s]** Final shot: the street falls silent under the dusk sky, the group standing together, exhausted but safe. Cut to black.
>
> Natural diegetic sound throughout — convulsing, screaming, crying, metal impacts, heavy breathing — layered with a tense instrumental score building through the chaos and easing into relief at the end. No text overlay, no watermark. Maximum dynamic camera variety — close-ups, wide shots, tracking shots, slow motion reserved for the final takedowns. Realistic shake only during physical impacts, natural dusk color grading.

Made with seedance 2.5 on @SocialSight
```

### Solo K-POP MV · Second-by-Second Y2K Candy World Storyboard

> A 30-second, second-by-second K-POP MV script that locks choreography, lip-sync, editing, and scenes to the audio’s actual beats. The protagonist’s face and outfit remain fixed across six Y2K settings, the number and positions of backup dancers are precisely controlled segment by segment, and the ending hard-stops as a flip phone snaps shut.

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-25-kpop-mv-zero-to-pop/poster.jpg" width="600" alt="Solo K-POP MV · Second-by-Second Y2K Candy World Storyboard">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-25-kpop-mv-zero-to-pop?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @aiwarts | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/aiwarts/status/2085736621685764503) | **公開日:** 2026-08-07 | **ヒート:** 82

**安定度:** 48/100

**再テスト:** MiniMax H3 Max 768p · 2026-09-07 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 47.5) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-25-kpop-mv-zero-to-pop/video-minimax-h3-768p-20260907-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（235 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
使用@音频1《Zero to Pop》作为唯一音乐、节拍和口型依据，生成一支30秒、16:9、24fps的原创单人K-POP MV。

视频必须完整覆盖音频的0.00–30.00秒，不得截断、延长、变速或自动淡出。不要重新生成背景音乐，不要加入另一首歌曲。

全片只有@图1是主角和主唱。允许出现2—6名伴舞，但伴舞不能获得面部特写、不能对口型、不能遮挡主角或成为第二视觉中心。

主角的脸、发型、发色、身材比例、服装和饰品全程锁定。只有主角按照@音频1中的实际人声精确对口型；没有人声的段落保持闭唇表演。

【整体视觉】

高预算真人K-POP MV，结合Y2K未来主义、怪诞糖果世界和千禧年数字文化：

CRT电子宠物、透明翻盖手机、像素图标、果冻便利店、充气卧室、镭射洗车通道、巨型CD舞台、镀铬金属、透明塑料和CD彩虹衍射。

色彩以泡泡糖粉、电光蓝、蓝莓紫、酸性绿和镀铬银为主。

怪诞元素必须像真正搭建的实景、机械装置和高级视觉特效，不能像廉价AI拼贴、普通夜店或传统赛博朋克城市。

【主角造型】

主角穿原创Y2K未来女团服装：

银白色不对称短夹克、糖果粉亮片内搭、蓝紫渐变低腰工装裤、透明树脂腰带、镀铬圆环饰品、银白厚底运动鞋，以及一个迷你透明翻盖手机挂饰。

服装全程不变。金属、塑料、布料、头发和饰品必须表现真实重量、反光、惯性和自然摆动。

【音频同步原则】

歌曲约128 BPM，每个主节拍间隔约0.469秒。

最重要的画面锁点：

2.78秒：第一个强重拍，正式进入舞蹈。
6.06秒：第一次换景。
9.82秒：进入翻盖手机表演镜头。
12.63秒：进入第一段收束动作。
14.02秒：音乐明显降能量，切入失重卧室。
17.78秒：节奏重新启动。
21.07秒：高潮副歌和巨型CD舞台出现。
24.82秒：音乐突然抽空，伴舞冻结。
28.95秒：最后一句和最终特写。
29.90—30.00秒：翻盖手机合上，画面硬停。

所有剪辑、甩发、转身、手势、踩地、队形变化和摄影机冲击都必须落在@音频1的真实重拍上。

【逐秒镜头时间线】

0.00–0.47秒：

黑暗中出现一条细窄的CRT扫描线，随歌曲第一个电子启动声闪亮。

不要出现人物，不要添加片名或文字。

0.47–1.88秒：

扫描线展开为一台圆角CRT显示器。

屏幕内出现一只原创像素电子宠物。它随着音频中的电子声睁开眼睛，头顶依次跳出像素爱心、电池和小星星。

所有图标只使用抽象像素图形，不要出现品牌、角色或乱码文字。

1.88–2.78秒：

摄影机快速从CRT屏幕中后退，揭示完整的半透明粉色街机房。

主角已经站在CRT前，侧身背对镜头。摄影机停稳时，她缓慢回头，眼神锁定镜头。

她必须在2.78秒第一个强重拍准确击中开场姿势。

2.78–6.06秒：

低机位鱼眼单人中近景。

主角进入第一段独舞：按键式手指动作、肩部弹动、手臂波浪、髋部重心切换和一次快速半转。

所有动作根据@音频1逐拍执行，不要机械地平均分配动作。

主角按照实际人声自然对口型。背景CRT里的像素宠物同步模仿她的节奏，但不能变成人类或复制她的脸。

摄影机围绕主角移动约45度，同时缓慢推进。

6.06秒，主角手掌拍向镜头，形成遮挡式硬切。

6.06–9.82秒：

进入外星果冻便利店。

主角站在粉色自动传送带中央，4名伴舞从两侧货架后滑入，形成主角在最前方的V形队列。

五人完成连续行进编舞：两步滑行、交叉手臂、肩部撞击、一次同步下沉和快速回弹。

主角距离摄影机最近，服装颜色最鲜明，面部始终清晰。伴舞动作统一但幅度略小。

摄影机正面后退，与传送带保持相同速度。

9.82–12.63秒：

中长焦主角单人镜头。

伴舞退到背景并失焦。主角从货架上拿起一只巨大的透明果冻翻盖手机。

她对着手机完成歌曲中这一段的实际对口型，通过眼神和嘴角表现出俏皮、略带嫌弃的态度。

手机按键根据音乐节拍发光。摄影机在强音上做两次短促punch zoom，但不改变主角身份或面部结构。

12.63–14.02秒：

切回全身镜头。

主角把果冻手机抛向空中，4名伴舞重新进入队形。

全员在三个主要节拍上依次完成：

第一拍：双臂向外展开；
第二拍：快速转身并甩发；
第三拍：身体下沉后锐利定格。

主角踩亮地面上的圆形像素按钮。一道蓝紫色扫描光从脚底向上扫过所有人物。

14.02秒，音乐降能量的瞬间完成场景转换。

14.02–15.55秒：

进入淡紫色失重充气卧室。

画面由快速舞蹈突然转为稳定、梦幻的长镜头。主角坐在充气床边，房间和家具开始缓慢旋转，但主角仍保持正常垂直方向。

不出现伴舞。

摄影机缓慢靠近。主角根据实际音频进行克制、贴近耳语感的对口型，不做大幅度舞蹈。

透明枕头、小型CD和银色耳机线缓慢漂浮。

15.55–17.78秒：

主角从床边站起，向摄影机走来。

两名伴舞出现在旋转房间的左右墙面，制造重力方向错乱的视觉效果，但不能抢夺主角注意力。

主角伸手触碰一张漂浮的小型CD。CD逐渐膨胀为彩虹色圆形入口。

17.78秒节奏重新出现时，主角迈步穿过CD入口，摄影机紧随她穿过，形成无缝转场。

17.78–21.07秒：

进入镭射泡泡洗车隧道。

节奏重新启动，主角带领4名伴舞向前完成行进式编舞：强力踏步、交叉手臂、头部转向、肩部重击和左右展开队形。

镀铬拱门、粉蓝旋转软刷和彩灯按照真实节拍运动。透明泡泡从人物之间经过，但不得遮挡主角面部。

摄影机在主角正前方快速后退，主角始终位于中心。

20.60–21.07秒之间，主角踢向一个巨大的透明泡泡。泡泡表面转化为CD彩虹纹理。

21.07秒高潮重拍时，泡泡爆开并完成换景。

21.07–24.82秒：

进入巨型CD终章舞台，这是全片能量最高的段落。

主角站在直径12米的旋转CD中心，6名伴舞组成对称半圆。摄影机从高位快速下降至略低于腰部的英雄角度。

主角按照歌曲高潮段落精确对口型，全员完成副歌核心编舞：

两拍快速手臂切换；
一次髋部重击；
一次同步旋转；
一次全身下沉；
最后伴舞向两侧打开，把舞台中心完全让给主角。

摄影机在主角旋转时绕行半圈，但不能越过轴线或造成左右翻转。

CD表面产生真实彩虹衍射，像素星空和镀铬行星随节奏闪动。

24.82秒音乐突然抽空时，所有伴舞立刻冻结。

24.82–27.00秒：

主角继续保持运动，伴舞像卡顿的电子游戏角色一样定格在背景。

主角独自向摄影机走两步。画面根据音频中的断奏出现克制的数字卡顿效果：同一个动作短暂重复1—2帧，但主角面部保持稳定。

随着音乐继续变空，冻结的伴舞依次化为方形像素颗粒并消失。

主角不得消失、复制或变形。

27.00–28.95秒：

舞台上只剩主角。

巨型CD停止旋转，背景灯光大部分熄灭。摄影机缓慢推近主角面部。

主角取下腰间的迷你透明翻盖手机，将它举到脸旁。她保持闭唇，直到@音频1最后一句人声真正开始。

画面运动变得极简，把注意力完全留给最后一句和主角表情。

28.95–29.90秒：

主角精确对口型歌曲最后一句。

她直视镜头，从冷静表情逐渐变成带有恶作剧感的轻微笑容。摄影机继续推进至面部特写。

只保留银粉色轮廓光和眼睛里的小型方形反光。

29.90–30.00秒：

主角"啪"地合上翻盖手机。

合盖动作必须与歌曲最终收尾音完全同步。背景灯光瞬间熄灭，画面硬切为黑色。

不要淡出，不要延长尾音，不要字幕、Logo或额外结束镜头。

【表演与口型】

只允许主角对口型。口型必须依据@音频1中的真实语音和节奏生成，不要根据文字自行猜测，不要在纯器乐段随机张嘴。

主角在快速舞蹈时仍保持自然呼吸、面部肌肉运动和稳定身份。表情变化需要与音乐能量一致：

前半段俏皮、自信；
14.02秒后变得梦幻、克制；
17.78秒重新充满力量；
21.07秒进入主打歌级舞台状态；
24.82秒后变得冷静、带恶作剧感。

【硬性限制】

不得改变@音频1的速度、结构或长度。
不得加入新的背景音乐、对白或无关人声。
音效只能作为非常轻微的视觉反馈，不能覆盖原曲。
主角从第一帧到最后一帧必须保持同一张脸。
不得生成主角克隆体或第二名主唱。
伴舞最多6名，面孔必须互不相同。
禁止人物融合、额外肢体、畸形手指、脚底漂移、错误倒影、服装突变和随机换景。
禁止乱码文字、品牌、商标、字幕和水印。
```

</details>

SCENE — WATERBENDING AMBUSH
DURATION: exactly 10 seconds
FORMAT: 16:9
STYLE: ultra-photorealistic live-action fantasy action, grounded CGI, realistic water physics, Hollywood stunt choreography, practical jungle-temple location, handheld cinematography, seamless VFX integration, no digital-art appearance.
PACE: extremely fast continuous combat, zero pauses, zero idle moments, action already in progress.

REFERENCES

IMAGE 1 — ARMAN
Preserve facial identity, hairstyle, body proportions, costume, and appearance exactly.

IMAGE 2 — MASTER ENVIRONMENT SHEET
Preserve environment, architecture, vegetation, atmosphere, and location identity exactly.

IMAGE 3 — ENEMY REFERENCE
Preserve enemy armor, silhouette, proportions, and visual identity exactly.

ACTION

0.0–2.5 SECONDS — SURROUNDED
Fight begins mid-combat. Five armored enemies attack from multiple directions. Arman pivots beneath a frontal strike while a large mass of water is already orbiting around him. He redirects the flow to deflect Enemy A. Several sections split from the moving water, instantly freeze into sharp ice shards, and fire toward attacking enemies. Camera performs fast handheld lateral tracking.

2.5–5.0 SECONDS — ROTATING PRESSURE
Enemy C closes from behind. Arman ducks beneath an attack and transitions into a continuous spinning movement. The main water mass whips around him with believable inertia, striking, deflecting, and creating openings. Multiple ice shards separate from the flowing water and launch toward different attackers, forcing evasions and interrupting attacks. Camera reacts with quick pans and target switching.

5.0–7.5 SECONDS — WATER IMPACT
Enemy A attacks again. Arman redirects the entire water mass into a powerful lateral strike. Water impact throws the enemy sideways onto wet stone. While the enemy falls, additional ice shards fire toward advancing attackers. Arman immediately turns toward the next threat without stopping.

7.5–10.0 SECONDS — COMBO CONTINUES
Enemies close from opposite directions. Arman performs rapid circular footwork while continuously controlling the moving water flow. Fresh ice shards split from the leading edge and fire toward multiple enemies. Enemy C charges directly into range. Arman pivots and accelerates the main water mass toward him.

END BEFORE IMPACT.

SMASH CUT. ACTION INTERRUPTED MID-COMBO. END.

VISUAL RULES

- Five active enemies remain engaged throughout.
- No enemy stands and watches.
- Combat never slows down.
- Water remains a single continuous moving mass.
- Ice shards separate from the moving water, instantly freeze, and launch as sharp translucent projectiles.
- No glowing effects, energy beams, neon trails, or anime aura.
- Grounded CGI integrated into live-action photography.
- Every action directly causes the next event.
- Continuous movement, zero pauses, zero dead frames.
- Final frame preserves active combat momentum for Scene 02.

CAMERA

Reactive handheld Hollywood action camera. Fast but readable movement, medium-wide coverage, brief wider shots for major impacts, natural motion blur, occasional water spray crossing lens. No floating virtual camera.

ENVIRONMENT

Ancient jungle temple ruins. Wet stone, moss, roots, ferns, waterfalls, dense tropical vegetation, humid atmosphere, realistic live-action location photography.

SOUND

Raw action only. Footsteps, armor movement, weapons cutting air, rushing water, splashes, ice projectiles, impact sounds, bodies hitting wet ground, distant waterfalls and jungle ambience.

NO MUSIC.
```

### The Little Moments That Make AI Feel Real

> It’s the little moments that make AI feel this real. Made with Seedance 2.5 PROMPT Create a 30-second, 1080p, 16:9 ultra-realistic early-2000s DV home video of …

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/276528e2bcc8.jpg" width="600" alt="The Little Moments That Make AI Feel Real">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-it-s-the-little-moments-that-make-ai-feel-this-real-57c748edf467?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @SimplyAnnisa | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/SimplyAnnisa/status/2096931238460178592) | **公開日:** 2026-09-07 | **ヒート:** 82

**安定度:** 86/100

**再テスト:** Grok Imagine · 2026-10-03 · ✅ 再現 (スコア 85.8) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-it-s-the-little-moments-that-make-ai-feel-this-real-57c748edf467/video-grok-imagine-20261003-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（91 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
It’s the little moments that make AI feel this real.

Made with Seedance 2.5

PROMPT

Create a 30-second, 1080p, 16:9 ultra-realistic early-2000s DV home video of a young Korean woman having an ordinary but unexpectedly memorable summer evening in an older Seoul neighborhood.

A naturally pretty Korean woman in her early 20s, realistic skin, minimal makeup, long black slightly wavy hair, pale-blue knit top, loose cream trousers, white sneakers, brown crossbody bag, and silver watch. Keep her identity, face, outfit, hairstyle, and proportions perfectly consistent.

SETTING

A quiet older Seoul residential street with concrete lanes, apartment buildings, potted plants, bicycles, utility poles, leafy trees, and a tiny neighborhood convenience shop. Lived-in and authentic. No brands, logos, advertisements, or tourist locations.

CAMERA STYLE

Raw footage from a cheap early-2000s DV camcorder: shaky handheld movement, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, exposure shifts, faded colors, soft detail, mild tape noise, accidental zooms, and natural camera mistakes. No stabilization or cinematic shots.

00:00–00:06 — THE MYSTERY

She walks down the street holding a small plastic bag when she suddenly hears a faint jingling sound behind her.

She stops and looks around.

The camera quickly zooms toward her confused face.

She says:

“Did you hear that?”

00:06–00:12 — THE LITTLE DISCOVERY

She follows the sound and finds a small old bicycle with a tiny bell stuck slightly open.

She gently taps the bell.

Ding.

She looks at the camera and laughs.

Then she notices a small handwritten-looking paper tag hanging from the bicycle, but the writing is too unclear to read.

00:12–00:18 — THE WIND

A sudden breeze blows several dry leaves across the street.

One lands directly on her head.

She doesn't notice.

The camera operator laughs.

She looks confused, then realizes and removes the leaf.

She gives the camera an embarrassed stare.

00:18–00:24 — THE SMALL CHALLENGE

She places the leaf on the bicycle seat and tries to balance it there.

The wind immediately blows it away.

She tries again.

It falls again.

She laughs and finally gives up.

00:24–00:30 — THE MEMORY

She picks up the leaf, puts it into her small bag, and starts walking home.

After a few steps, she turns toward the camera and says:

“Okay, that was pointless.”

She smiles and keeps walking.

The camera follows her for a few seconds before abruptly cutting to black.

AUDIO

Natural location sound only: footsteps, distant traffic, bicycle bell, summer insects, leaves, wind, neighborhood ambience, and natural laughter.

No music, narration, subtitles, captions, logos, watermarks, or on-screen text.

REALISM

Natural human reactions, imperfect timing, realistic physics, consistent objects, authentic Korean neighborhood details, and believable DV-camera imperfections. No CGI look, distorted hands, extra fingers, duplicated people, identity drift, or outfit changes.

16:9 aspect ratio.
```

</details>

Candid domestic humor, realistic fabric movement, nostalgic DV imperfections and believable everyday chaos.

### Nano Banana + Seedance 2.5 High School First Love Film

> Maybe it was never about the five minutes. It was about wanting to spend them together. ❤️ Made with Flova AI using Nano

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/media/poster/caden-flux-seedance-ai-8ffb5f062951.jpg" width="600" alt="Nano Banana + Seedance 2.5 High School First Love Film">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/caden-flux-seedance-ai-8ffb5f062951?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Caden_Flux | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Caden_Flux/status/2089154775829074074) | **公開日:** 2026-08-17 | **ヒート:** 82

**安定度:** 75/100

**再テスト:** MiniMax H3 Max 768p · 2026-09-07 · ✅ 再現 (スコア 75) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/caden-flux-seedance-ai-8ffb5f062951/video-minimax-h3-768p-20260907-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（363 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Create a 60-second cinematic high-school first-love story using SEEDANCE 2.5.

IMPORTANT:
Generate the video as EXACTLY TWO CONTINUOUS SHOTS:
SHOT 1 = 0–30 seconds
SHOT 2 = 30–60 seconds
The two shots must connect seamlessly and feel like ONE continuous short film, not two unrelated clips.
FORMAT:
16:9 widescreen
Cinematic photorealism
Premium coming-of-age romance film
Fast-paced but emotionally natural
Smooth transitions and motivated camera movement
Realistic teenage body proportions and expressions
Natural English dialogue only
No subtitles unless dialogue is naturally spoken
No text overlays
No character redesign
CHARACTER CONSISTENCY — EXTREMELY IMPORTANT:
Use the provided character reference sheets for BOTH characters.

BOY:
Preserve exact face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, accessories and identity from the character sheet.

GIRL:
Preserve exact face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, accessories and identity from the character sheet.
Their faces, hairstyles and outfits must remain IDENTICAL throughout both shots.
The boy and girl are both 17–18-year-old high-school students. Keep the romance completely innocent, wholesome and age-appropriate.
VISUAL LANGUAGE:
Warm nostalgic coming-of-age movie aesthetic, realistic school environment, natural skin texture, subtle film grain, cinematic depth of field, soft lens bloom, realistic physics, natural hair movement, expressive eyes, believable teenage body language.
Use a dynamic combination of:
wide establishing shots,
medium tracking shots,
over-the-shoulder shots,
close-ups,
handheld intimate moments,
smooth push-ins,
whip-pan transitions,
shallow-focus inserts,
and brief slow-motion accents.
Avoid excessive slow motion. The pacing should feel energetic and modern.
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
SHOT 1 — 0:00–0:30
“THE ROUTINE”
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
0:00–0:03
OPEN on a cinematic wide shot of a lively high-school hallway immediately after the final bell.
Students rush past the camera.
The boy exits his classroom with his backpack over one shoulder.
He looks across the hallway.

The girl is already there.
Their eyes meet.

She gives him a tiny smile.
He smiles back.

CAMERA:
Fast lateral tracking shot through the crowd, then smoothly pushes toward the boy as he notices her.
0:03–0:07
She walks toward him.

He casually falls into step beside her.

GIRL:
“You're late.”

BOY:
“By two minutes.”
She laughs.

They continue walking.
CAMERA:
Smooth backward tracking shot in front of them as students pass naturally around them.
0:07–0:11
QUICK MONTAGE.
They sit together in class.
She steals one of his fries during lunch.
He looks offended.
She laughs.
He secretly smiles.
CUT TO:
Their notebooks side by side.
She draws a tiny smiley face in the margin of his notebook.

He notices.

CAMERA:
Fast close-up inserts and match cuts, keeping the rhythm playful and energetic.
0:11–0:15
They leave school together.
Golden afternoon light floods the corridor.
He holds the door open for her.

She playfully bumps his shoulder as she walks past.
He laughs.

CAMERA:
Low-angle tracking shot transitioning into a warm side-profile shot.

0:15–0:20
Outside school.

They walk down the sidewalk.

She talks animatedly while he listens.

A light breeze moves her hair.

He looks at her for a moment longer than he should.

She catches him staring.

GIRL:
“What?”
BOY:
“Nothing.”

She smiles knowingly.
CAMERA:
Over-the-shoulder close-up from behind her, revealing his shy smile.

0:20–0:25
The sky suddenly darkens.

First drops of rain hit the pavement.
She looks upward.

BOY:
“Uh-oh.”
She laughs and quickly pulls her cardigan closer.

The boy opens his small umbrella.

CAMERA:
Quick tilt from the darkening sky down to them.

0:25–0:30
They squeeze underneath the tiny umbrella together.
Their shoulders bump.

They laugh.
The camera slowly circles around them as they begin walking through the rain.

The boy looks at her.

She looks back.

A brief quiet moment.

MATCH CUT:
Camera passes behind the umbrella fabric.

Use the movement of the umbrella to create a seamless transition into SHOT 2.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
SHOT 2 — 0:30–1:00
“FIVE MORE MINUTES”
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
IMPORTANT:
SHOT 2 begins from the EXACT SAME MOMENT as Shot 1 ends.

Same characters.
Same clothing.
Same umbrella.
Same rainy street.
Same lighting.
Same environment.

No visual reset.

0:30–0:35
Continue the walking shot.
They move through the rain beneath the tiny umbrella.

Their hands accidentally touch.
Both notice.

Neither pulls away immediately.

CAMERA:
Slow subtle push-in toward their hands, then rack focus to their faces.

0:35–0:40
They reach the girl's house.

She steps out from beneath the umbrella.

She turns toward him.
GIRL:
“See you tomorrow?”

BOY:
“Yeah.”
She starts walking toward her front door.

The boy watches her.

She takes a few steps.

0:40–0:44
He suddenly gathers courage.

BOY:
“Hey!”

She turns around.

CAMERA:
Quick handheld push toward the boy as he runs a few steps back toward her.
0:44–0:49
He smiles nervously.

BOY:
“Can I have five more minutes?”
She looks at him, amused.

GIRL:
“You already had an hour.”

He laughs.
BOY:
“I know.”

A small pause.
She smiles.

GIRL:
“Okay. Five more minutes.”

0:49–0:54
She steps back underneath the umbrella.

They begin walking together again.
The rain becomes softer.

The street lights begin glowing as evening approaches.

CAMERA:
Wide cinematic tracking shot from the front, slowly moving backward as they walk toward camera.

0:54–0:58
QUICK NOSTALGIC MONTAGE:
Their sneakers splashing through puddles.

Their hands almost touching.
Her laughing.

His nervous smile.

The tiny umbrella tilting as they walk closer together.

A final glance between them.

Use rhythmic match cuts synced to the emotional music.

0:58–1:00
FINAL WIDE SHOT.

The camera pulls slowly upward and backward.

The two teenagers walk away together beneath the tiny umbrella, becoming smaller against the glowing evening street.
The rain sparkles under the streetlights.

They continue talking and laughing as they disappear farther down the road.

FADE OUT.
FINAL EMOTIONAL FEELING:
First love.
Youth.
Nervous butterflies.
The feeling of not wanting the day to end.
Do NOT make the ending sad.
Do NOT introduce a breakup.
Do NOT introduce a twist.
Do NOT introduce additional characters who become romantically relevant.
The entire story should feel like one precious memory from the beginning of a first love.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
CAMERA & MOTION REQUIREMENTS
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Keep camera movement fluid and cinematic throughout.

Use motivated transitions rather than random cuts.
Mix fast-paced montage editing with slower intimate close-ups.
Prioritize:
• smooth tracking shots
• natural handheld movement
• cinematic push-ins
• over-the-shoulder compositions
• expressive close-ups
• realistic rack focus
• wide environmental shots
• subtle slow-motion only for emotional beats
Maintain realistic physics for:
rain,
umbrella movement,
hair,
clothing,
walking,
running,
hand gestures,
and interactions with the environment.
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
AUDIO & DIALOGUE
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
Natural English dialogue only.

Dialogue should sound spontaneous and age-appropriate.

Ambient sound:
school hallway chatter,
footsteps,
distant laughter,
birds,
city ambience,
rain,
umbrella fabric,
wet pavement.
Music:
soft modern coming-of-age instrumental soundtrack that gradually builds from playful and light to warm and emotional.

Music must never overpower dialogue.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
NEGATIVE PROMPT
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

No character identity changes.
No face changes.
No hairstyle changes.
No outfit changes.
No age changes.
No adult appearance.
No sexualized content.
No exaggerated body proportions.
No random wardrobe changes.
No duplicated characters.
No extra limbs or fingers.
No distorted hands.
No unnatural walking.
No floating objects.
No impossible rain physics.
No inconsistent umbrella.
No teleporting.
No location jumps that break continuity.
No random camera cuts.
No excessive slow motion.
No cartoon appearance.
No anime style.
No plastic skin.
No overprocessed faces.
No text overlays.
No subtitles.
No logos.
No watermark.
No visual glitches.
No abrupt ending.

FINAL REQUIREMENT:
SEEDANCE 2.5 MUST PRIORITIZE CHARACTER CONSISTENCY, FACIAL IDENTITY, TEMPORAL CONTINUITY, NATURAL MOTION AND SEAMLESS TRANSITION BETWEEN THE TWO 30-SECOND SHOTS.

The final result should look like a polished 60-second scene from a premium cinematic coming-of-age romance film.
```

### Daily Rural Life of a Young Woman in 19th-Century Tuscany

> This AI video is a Masterpiece 👏 Seedance 2.5 Prompt: 30-second ultra-realistic documentary slice-of-life video, early-2000s consumer DV camcorder aesthetic. A…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/d2a428b7016a.jpg" width="600" alt="Daily Rural Life of a Young Woman in 19th-Century Tuscany">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-30-second-ultra-realistic-documentary-slice-of-life-video-early-2000s-consumer-c3a9eabe6881?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Goodmanprotocol | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Goodmanprotocol/status/2107530733212590250) | **公開日:** 2026-10-06 | **ヒート:** 81

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（20 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
30-second ultra-realistic documentary slice-of-life video, early-2000s consumer DV camcorder aesthetic. A young Italian peasant woman in her early 20s, sun-weathered olive skin with freckles, dark brown wavy hair loosely tied with a faded scarf, worn off-white linen blouse, earth-brown patched wool skirt, faded floral apron and muddy leather boots. Rural Tuscany in the late 1800s. Golden wheat fields, olive trees, stone farmhouse, cypress trees, stone walls, chickens, donkey and old stone well. No modern objects or machinery.

[0–6s]
Handheld camera follows her naturally along a dirt path through golden wheat fields. She carries a woven basket on her hip and brushes her free hand across the wheat. Wind moves the stalks and her loose hair.

[6–12s]
She reaches a wooden fence, sets down the basket and feeds scraps to several chickens gathered around her boots. The camera stays close and imperfect, briefly adjusting focus as she moves.

[12–18s]
She continues toward an old stone well. She lowers a wooden bucket on a rope, pulls it up with effort, then drinks fresh water from a small ladle. Water runs down her chin and she wipes it away with her sleeve.

[18–24s]
A donkey approaches. She smiles softly, pats its muzzle, then picks a small wildflower from the field and tucks it behind her ear. She lifts the basket and walks toward the farmhouse.

[24–30s]
Near the heavy wooden doorway, she notices the person filming. She gives a tired but warm smile and a small wave, then opens the door. She looks back once before stepping inside. The camera remains outside as the door slowly closes.

STYLE: photorealistic historical documentary, authentic 1800s Tuscany, early-2000s consumer DV look, handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, slight exposure pumping, faded colors, soft contrast, mild digital compression and sensor noise. No stabilization, no cinematic camera moves, no modern color grading. One continuous fluid shot, no cuts, no captions, no music.

AUDIO: natural ambience only -wind through wheat, birds, chickens, footsteps on dry earth, rope and wooden bucket creaking, water splashing, donkey sounds, distant countryside atmosphere and the wooden door closing.
```

</details>

Setting: A cozy modern indoor room with warm ambient lighting, stylish wall posters, a hanging pendant lamp, and contemporary furniture, filmed with a cinematic handheld camera look.

Subject: A young woman with long dark hair wearing a gray oversized hooded jacket performs a smooth, rhythmic choreographed hand-and-body dance directly in front of the camera. Her movements are expressive, precise, and naturally synchronized with the visual effects.

The holographic worlds continuously morph, rotate, and expand with every gesture. Digital particles flow between the real and virtual environments, creating seamless transitions where parts of the futuristic world briefly appear in the real room before dissolving back into reality.

Ending: The final geometric frame closes around her face, the holographic effects rapidly fade away, and everything smoothly returns to the original cozy room, leaving only subtle glowing particles as she finishes her final pose.

### A Morning Kimchi Stew Stop at a Seoul Neighborhood Eatery

> This AI video made me question what “AI-looking” even means anymore. Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI Create a 15-second ultra-realistic early-2000s Sony MiniDV home-…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/89ff10c2393b.jpg" width="600" alt="A Morning Kimchi Stew Stop at a Seoul Neighborhood Eatery">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-this-ai-video-made-me-question-what-ai-looking-even-means-anymore-c3cc664055dd?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Just_sharon7 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Just_sharon7/status/2104819017655648545) | **公開日:** 2026-09-29 | **ヒート:** 81

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（224 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
This AI video made me question what “AI-looking” even means anymore.

Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI 

Create a 15-second ultra-realistic early-2000s Sony MiniDV home-video of a young Korean woman stopping at a small neighborhood Korean restaurant during a quiet morning in Seoul.

MAIN SUBJECT

Young Korean woman, 24 years old, naturally attractive with realistic skin texture, minimal makeup, expressive dark-brown eyes and long dark hair worn loosely down.

Preserve her exact identity, facial features, hairstyle, body proportions and appearance throughout the entire video.

OUTFIT

Replace the maroon hoodie with a cozy casual early-2000s outfit:

soft cream cable-knit cardigan
muted dusty-blue fitted T-shirt underneath
loose dark-brown straight-leg trousers
worn white canvas sneakers
small dark-brown canvas shoulder bag
simple silver wristwatch

Her clothing should look ordinary, comfortable and naturally lived-in rather than styled for a fashion shoot.

LOCATION — SMALL KOREAN RESTAURANT

A tiny family-run Korean restaurant tucked into an older residential neighborhood in Seoul.

The restaurant feels authentic and modest rather than trendy.

Outside are an aged Korean signboard, slightly faded menu posters, a small standing menu board, plastic beverage crates, potted plants, an old bicycle leaning against the wall and a glass entrance door covered with a few small Korean stickers.

Inside, show glimpses of:

compact wooden tables
metal chopstick containers
stainless-steel cups
tissue boxes
condiment bottles
handwritten Korean menu sheets
an old wall clock
small refrigerator filled with drinks
steaming pots behind the counter
an older Korean woman preparing food

Warm steam occasionally drifts from the kitchen.

The restaurant should feel like a genuine neighborhood place that local residents have visited for years.

LIGHTING & ATMOSPHERE

Quiet early morning in Seoul.

Soft overcast daylight outside has a cool blue-gray tone, while the restaurant interior glows with warm yellow fluorescent and tungsten light.

This creates a cozy contrast between the cool street and warm restaurant.

Muted colors, slightly underexposed shadows, soft highlights and imperfect white balance.

A gentle breeze moves her loose hair and cardigan when she is outside.

The atmosphere is peaceful, nostalgic, comfortable and slightly sleepy.

CAMERA STYLE

Ultra-realistic early-2000s Sony MiniDV home video, filmed casually by another person accompanying her.

Completely candid and unstaged.

Natural handheld operation with:

subtle hand shake
imperfect framing
occasional crooked composition
gentle accidental reframing
slight autofocus hunting
small exposure adjustments
imperfect automatic white balance
faded MiniDV colors
soft contrast
mild highlight blooming
authentic DV compression
subtle digital noise
slight microphone hiss
occasional autofocus breathing

No stabilization. No cinematic gimbal movement. No modern commercial color grading.

Motion remains fluid and natural at normal real-time speed.

00:00–00:03 — ARRIVING

The camcorder follows slightly behind her as she approaches the tiny neighborhood restaurant.

She notices the faded menu displayed beside the entrance and slows down.

She leans slightly toward it, reading the Korean dishes.

A little steam escapes when another customer opens the restaurant door.

She smiles and quietly says:

WOMAN:
“아… 배고프다.”

She lightly touches her stomach and looks through the restaurant window.
00:03–00:06 — LOOKING AT THE MENU

She steps closer to the handwritten menu board.

Her canvas bag slips slightly down her shoulder while she reads.

She casually pushes it back up without looking.

The person filming asks from behind the camcorder:

CAMERA PERSON:
“뭐 먹을래?”

Without turning around, she continues studying the menu.

WOMAN:
“잠깐만…”

She points at one item with her finger.
00:06–00:09 — INSIDE

Natural handheld cut as she pushes open the glass restaurant door.

A small bell above the door jingles.
The warm restaurant interior contrasts with the cool morning outside.

An older woman behind the counter looks up.

RESTAURANT OWNER:
“어서 오세요.”

The young woman gives a small polite bow.

WOMAN:
“안녕하세요.”

She walks toward an empty table.

00:09–00:12 — THE LITTLE ACCIDENT

She sits at the small wooden table and begins removing her shoulder bag.

Without noticing, her elbow lightly bumps the stainless-steel chopstick container.
It tilts.

She reacts quickly and catches it with both hands just before it falls over.

Her eyes widen for a second.
She freezes.

Then she slowly looks directly toward the camcorder.

The person filming quietly laughs.

CAMERA PERSON:
“야…”

She immediately starts laughing too.

WOMAN:
“안 떨어졌잖아.”

Her expression is playfully defensive and slightly embarrassed.

00:12–00:15 — FOOD ARRIVES

A steaming bowl of kimchi-jjigae is placed in front of her with rice and several small banchan dishes.

Steam rises naturally toward her face.

Her embarrassed expression immediately changes into a delighted smile.

She leans slightly toward the bowl and inhales the aroma.

WOMAN:
“와… 맛있겠다.”
She picks up her metal spoon, then briefly looks toward the camcorder with an excited little smile.

The camera accidentally drifts downward toward the steaming stew before casually reframing her face.

End naturally as she begins eating.
AUDIO

Natural location audio only.

Include:

soft neighborhood traffic, distant bicycle bell, footsteps, restaurant door bell, quiet Korean conversation in the background, kitchen utensils, bubbling stew, plates touching tables, metal chopsticks, refrigerator hum, ventilation fan, chair movement, fabric rustling and subtle MiniDV microphone hiss.

No music. No narration. No artificial sound effects.

MOTION & REALISM

All movement must remain continuous, physically believable and real-time.

The cardigan, hair and shoulder bag should have realistic secondary motion.

Steam from the food should rise irregularly and naturally.
When she accidentally knocks the chopstick container, it should only tilt slightly and she should realistically catch it—no exaggerated slapstick reaction.

Her expressions should transition naturally:

sleepy curiosity → hunger → concentration → surprise → embarrassment → laughter → excitement when the food arrives.

The humor should come from an extremely ordinary human moment rather than staged comedy.

IMPORTANT

The video should feel like someone found an old MiniDV tape from a casual morning in Seoul around 2003–2005.

Do not make the restaurant luxurious, modern, minimalist or Instagram-style.

Avoid perfect compositions, dramatic cinematic lighting, beauty filters, overly clean skin, exaggerated acting, artificial slow motion, speed ramps, frame skipping, duplicated frames, stop-motion movement or excessive motion blur.

The final glance at the camcorder should feel spontaneous and familiar, as though she is comfortable with the person filming her.

The vintage appearance must come from realistic MiniDV camera behavior, autofocus, automatic exposure, white-balance imperfections, compression and casual handheld operation, not from choppy or artificially degraded motion.
```

</details>
